   124→def triage_phase_banner(plan: PlanModel) -> str:
   125→    """Return a banner string when triage stage IDs are in the queue."""
   126→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   127→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   128→    has_triage = any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS)
   129→    if not has_triage:
   130→        return ""
   131→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   132→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   133→    completed = [s for s in ("observe", "reflect", "organize") if s in stages]
   134→    if completed:
   135→        return (
   136→            f"TRIAGE MODE ({len(completed)}/4 stages complete) — "
   137→            "complete all stages to exit. Run: desloppify plan triage"
   138→        )
   139→    return (
   140→        "TRIAGE MODE — review issues need analysis before fixing. "
   141→        "Run: desloppify plan triage"
   142→    )
   143→
   144→

   145→

   158→
   159→
   160→class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
   161→    version: Required[int]
   162→    created: Required[str]
   163→    updated: Required[str]
   164→    queue_order: list[str]
   165→    deferred: list[str]  # kept empty for migration compat
   166→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]
   167→    active_cluster: str | None
   168→    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]
   169→    clusters: dict[str, Cluster]
   170→    superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]
   171→    promoted_ids: list[str]  # IDs user explicitly positioned via move_items()
   172→    plan_start_scores: PlanStartScores
   173→    execution_log: list[ExecutionLogEntry]
   174→    epic_triage_meta: EpicTriageMeta
   175→    commit_log: list[CommitRecord]
   176→    uncommitted_issues: list[str]
   177→    commit_tracking_branch: str | None
   178→    completed_clusters: NotRequired[list[dict[str, Any]]]  # legacy snapshot key
   179→    # Legacy compat keys (consumed by migration helpers)
   180→    epics: NotRequired[dict[str, Any]]
   181→    epic_synthesis_meta: NotRequired[dict[str, Any]]
   182→    pending_plan_gate: NotRequired[bool]
   183→    uncommitted_findings: NotRequired[list[str]]
   184→
   185→
   186→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
   187→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
   188→    now = utc_now()
   189→    return {
   190→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
   191→        "created": now,
   192→        "updated": now,
   193→        "queue_order": [],
   194→        "deferred": [],
   195→        "skipped": {},
   196→        "active_cluster": None,
   197→        "overrides": {},
   198→        "clusters": {},
   199→        "superseded": {},
   200→        "promoted_ids": [],
   201→        "plan_start_scores": {},
   202→        "execution_log": [],
   203→        "epic_triage_meta": {},
   204→        "commit_log": [],
   205→        "uncommitted_issues": [],
   206→        "commit_tracking_branch": None,
   207→    }
   208→
   209→
   210→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   211→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
   212→
   213→    Handles migration from v1 (deferred list) to v2 (skipped dict),
   214→    v3 (top-level epics) to v4 (epics unified into clusters),
   215→    v5 (gates) to v6 (unified queue),
   216→    and v6 (synthesis naming) to v7 (triage naming).
   217→    """
   218→    defaults = empty_plan()
   219→    for key, value in defaults.items():
   220→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
   221→
   222→    _ensure_container_types(plan)
   223→    _migrate_deferred_to_skipped(plan)
   224→    _migrate_epics_to_clusters(plan)
   225→    _normalize_cluster_defaults(plan)
   226→    _migrate_v5_to_v6(plan)
   227→    _migrate_synthesis_to_triage(plan)
   228→
   229→
   230→def triage_clusters(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Cluster]:
   231→    """Return clusters whose name starts with ``EPIC_PREFIX``."""
   232→    return {
   233→        name: cluster
   234→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   235→        if name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX)
   236→    }
   237→
   238→
   239→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   240→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   241→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   242→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   243→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   244→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   245→
   246→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   247→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   248→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   249→    if overlap:
   250→        raise ValueError(
   251→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   252→        )
   253→
   254→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   255→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   256→        kind = entry.get("kind")
   257→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   258→            raise ValueError(
   259→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   260→            )
   261→
   262→
   263→__all__ = [
   264→    "EPIC_PREFIX",
   265→    "EpicTriageMeta",
   266→    "ExecutionLogEntry",
   267→    "PLAN_VERSION",
   268→    "Cluster",
   269→    "CommitRecord",
   270→    "ItemOverride",
   271→    "LastTriageSnapshot",
   272→    "PlanModel",
   273→    "PlanStartScores",
   274→    "SkipEntry",
   275→    "SupersededEntry",
   276→    "TriageStagePayload",
   277→    "VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS",
   278→    "VALID_SKIP_KINDS",
   279→    "empty_plan",
   280→    "ensure_plan_defaults",
   281→    "triage_clusters",
   282→    "validate_plan",
   283→]
   284→

1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — prepend never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
     6→  to the *front* of the queue unconditionally (onboarding priority).
     7→- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to
     8→  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    13→
    14→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan import stale_policy as stale_policy_mod
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.promoted_ids import promoted_insertion_index
    17→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    18→from desloppify.engine._plan.subjective_policy import (
    19→    NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS as _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS,
    20→    SubjectiveVisibility,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    23→
    24→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    25→TRIAGE_ID = "triage::pending"  # deprecated, kept for migration
    26→
    27→TRIAGE_PREFIX = "triage::"
    28→TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS = (
    29→    "triage::observe",
    30→    "triage::reflect",
    31→    "triage::organize",
    32→    "triage::commit",
    33→)
    34→TRIAGE_IDS = set(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS)
    35→WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID = "workflow::create-plan"
    36→WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID = "workflow::score-checkpoint"
    37→WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID = "workflow::import-scores"
    38→WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID = "workflow::communicate-score"
    39→WORKFLOW_PREFIX = "workflow::"
    40→SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES = ("triage::", "workflow::", "subjective::")
    41→
    42→
    43→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    44→# Result dataclasses
    45→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    46→
    47→@dataclass
    48→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    49→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    50→
    51→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    52→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    53→
    54→    @property
    55→    def changes(self) -> int:
    56→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    57→
    58→
    59→@dataclass
    60→class UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
    61→    """What changed during an unscored-dimension sync."""
    62→
    63→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    64→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    65→
    66→    @property
    67→    def changes(self) -> int:
    68→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    69→
    70→
    71→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    72→# ID helpers
    73→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    74→
    75→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    76→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    77→    return stale_policy_mod.current_stale_ids(
    78→        state,
    79→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    80→    )
    81→
    82→
    83→def current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    84→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    85→
    86→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` first; when that dict is empty
    87→    (common before any reviews have been run), falls through to
    88→    ``dimension_scores`` which carries placeholder metadata from scan.
    89→    """
    90→    return stale_policy_mod.current_unscored_ids(
    91→        state,
    92→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    93→    )
    94→
    95→
    96→def current_under_target_ids(
    97→    state: StateModel,
    98→    *,
    99→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   100→) -> set[str]:
   101→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are under target but not stale or unscored.
   102→
   103→    These are dimensions whose assessment is still current (not needing refresh)
   104→    but whose score hasn't reached the target yet.
   105→    """
   106→    return stale_policy_mod.current_under_target_ids(
   107→        state,
   108→        target_strict=target_strict,
   109→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
   110→    )
   111→
   112→
   113→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   114→# Unscored dimension sync (front of queue, unconditional)
   115→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   116→
   117→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   118→    plan: PlanModel,
   119→    state: StateModel,
   120→) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
   121→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   122→
   123→    1. **Prune** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are
   124→       no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs — that is
   125→       ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).
   126→    2. **Inject** — unconditionally prepend currently-unscored IDs to the
   127→       *front* of ``queue_order`` so initial reviews are the first priority.
   128→    """
   129→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   130→    result = UnscoredDimensionSyncResult()
   131→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   132→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   133→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   134→
   135→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
   136→    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
   137→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   138→        fid for fid in order
   139→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   140→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   141→        and fid not in stale_ids
   142→    ]
   143→    for fid in to_remove:
   144→        order.remove(fid)
   145→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   146→
   147→    # --- Inject: prepend unscored IDs after any promoted items -------------
   148→    existing = set(order)
   149→    insert_at = promoted_insertion_index(order, plan)
   150→    for uid in reversed(sorted(unscored_ids)):
   151→        if uid not in existing:
   152→            order.insert(insert_at, uid)
   153→            result.injected.append(uid)
   154→
   155→    return result
   156→
   157→
   158→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   159→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   160→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   161→
   162→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   163→    plan: PlanModel,
   164→    state: StateModel,
   165→    *,
   166→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   167→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   168→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
   169→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale and under-target subjective dimensions.
   170→
   171→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   172→       longer stale/under-target and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned
   173→       by ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   174→    2. Inject stale and under-target dimension IDs when either:
   175→       a. No objective items remain (mid-cycle: append to back), OR
   176→       b. A cycle just completed (post-cycle: insert at front so subjective
   177→          review takes priority over new objective issues).
   178→    """
   179→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   180→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
   181→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   182→    under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state)
   183→    injectable_ids = stale_ids | under_target_ids
   184→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   185→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   186→
   187→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
   188→    # Only prune IDs that are no longer injectable and not unscored.
   189→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   190→        fid for fid in order
   191→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   192→        and fid not in injectable_ids
   193→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   194→    ]
   195→    for fid in to_remove:
   196→        order.remove(fid)
   197→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   198→
   199→    # --- Inject or evict stale + under-target dimensions -----------------
   200→    if policy is not None:
   201→        has_real_items = policy.has_objective_backlog
   202→    else:
   203→        has_real_items = any(
   204→            f.get("status") == "open"
   205→            and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   206→            and not f.get("suppressed")
   207→            for f in state.get("issues", {}).values()
   208→        )
   209→
   210→    should_inject = not has_real_items or cycle_just_completed
   211→
   212→    if not should_inject:
   213→        # Mid-cycle with objective backlog: evict any stale/under-target IDs
   214→        # that are present in the queue.  They may have been grandfathered from
   215→        # the unscored phase and should not be visible until the objective
   216→        # backlog clears or a cycle completes.
   217→        to_evict = [
   218→            fid for fid in order
   219→            if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   220→            and fid in injectable_ids
   221→        ]
   222→        for fid in to_evict:
   223→            order.remove(fid)
   224→            result.pruned.append(fid)
   225→
   226→    if should_inject and injectable_ids:
   227→        existing = set(order)
   228→        if cycle_just_completed and has_real_items:
   229→            # Post-cycle: front-of-queue after promoted items so subjective
   230→            # review happens before the new objective cycle begins.
   231→            insert_at = promoted_insertion_index(order, plan)
   232→            for sid in reversed(sorted(injectable_ids)):
   233→                if sid not in existing:
   234→                    order.insert(insert_at, sid)
   235→                    result.injected.append(sid)
   236→        else:
   237→            # Mid-cycle or no objective backlog: append to back.
   238→            for sid in sorted(injectable_ids):
   239→                if sid not in existing:
   240→                    order.append(sid)
   241→                    result.injected.append(sid)
   242→
   243→    return result
   244→
   245→
   246→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   247→# Triage snapshot hash + sync
   248→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   249→
   250→def review_issue_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   251→    """Hash open review issue IDs to detect changes.
   252→
   253→    Returns empty string when there are no open review issues.
   254→    """
   255→    return stale_policy_mod.review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   256→
   257→
   258→@dataclass
   259→class TriageSyncResult:
   260→    """What changed during a triage sync."""
   261→
   262→    injected: bool = False
   263→    pruned: bool = False
   264→
   265→    @property
   266→    def changes(self) -> int:
   267→        return int(self.injected) + int(self.pruned)
   268→
   269→
   270→def sync_triage_needed(
   271→    plan: PlanModel,
   272→    state: StateModel,
   273→) -> TriageSyncResult:
   274→    """Inject 4 triage stage IDs at front of queue when review issues change.
   275→
   276→    Only injects stages not already confirmed in ``epic_triage_meta``.
   277→
   278→    When stages are already present but all new issues have been resolved
   279→    since injection, auto-prunes the stale stages and updates the hash.
   280→
   281→    When issues are *resolved* (current IDs are a subset of previously
   282→    triaged IDs), the snapshot hash is updated silently — no re-triage
   283→    is needed since the user is working through the plan.
   284→    """
   285→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   286→    result = TriageSyncResult()
   287→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   288→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   289→    confirmed = set(meta.get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   290→
   291→    # Check if any triage stage is already in queue
   292→    already_present = any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS)
   293→
   294→    current_hash = review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   295→    last_hash = meta.get("issue_snapshot_hash", "")
   296→
   297→    if already_present:
   298→        # Stages present — check if the reason for injection still applies.
   299→        # Only auto-prune when triage was completed before (hash exists),
   300→        # all new issues have been resolved, and no triage work is in
   301→        # progress.  This avoids pruning the initial triage or a
   302→        # user-started triage session.
   303→        if last_hash and not confirmed:
   304→            issues = state.get("issues", {})
   305→            current_review_ids = {
   306→                fid for fid, f in issues.items()
   307→                if f.get("status") == "open"
   308→                and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   309→            }
   310→            triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   311→            new_since_triage = current_review_ids - triaged_ids
   312→
   313→            if not new_since_triage:
   314→                # No new issues remain — prune stale stages
   315→                for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
   316→                    while sid in order:
   317→                        order.remove(sid)
   318→                if current_hash:
   319→                    meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   320→                    plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   321→                result.pruned = True
   322→        return result
   323→
   324→    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash:
   325→        # Distinguish "new issues appeared" from "issues were resolved".
   326→        # Only re-triage when genuinely new issues exist.
   327→        issues = state.get("issues", {})
   328→        current_review_ids = {
   329→            fid for fid, f in issues.items()
   330→            if f.get("status") == "open"
   331→            and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   332→        }
   333→        triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   334→        new_since_triage = current_review_ids - triaged_ids
   335→
   336→        if new_since_triage:
   337→            # New review issues appeared — re-triage needed
   338→            insert_at = promoted_insertion_index(order, plan)
   339→            stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   340→            existing = set(order)
   341→            injected_count = 0
   342→            for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
   343→                if name not in confirmed and sid not in existing:
   344→                    order.insert(insert_at + injected_count, sid)
   345→                    injected_count += 1
   346→            if injected_count:
   347→                result.injected = True
   348→        else:
   349→            # Only resolved issues changed the hash — update silently
   350→            meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   351→            plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   352→
   353→    return result
   354→
   355→
   356→@dataclass
   357→class ScoreCheckpointSyncResult:
   358→    """What changed during a score-checkpoint sync."""
   359→
   360→    injected: bool = False
   361→
   362→    @property
   363→    def changes(self) -> int:
   364→        return int(self.injected)
   365→
   366→
   367→def sync_score_checkpoint_needed(
   368→    plan: PlanModel,
   369→    state: StateModel,
   370→    *,
   371→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   372→) -> ScoreCheckpointSyncResult:
   373→    """Inject ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` when all initial reviews complete.
   374→
   375→    Injects when:
   376→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   377→    - ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` is not already in the queue
   378→
   379→    Positioned after subjective items but before triage/create-plan
   380→    so the user sees their updated strict score right after reviews finish.
   381→    """
   382→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   383→    result = ScoreCheckpointSyncResult()
   384→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   385→
   386→    if WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID in order:
   387→        return result
   388→
   389→    # Check that no unscored dimensions remain
   390→    if policy is not None:
   391→        if policy.unscored_ids:
   392→            return result
   393→    else:
   394→        unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   395→        if unscored:
   396→            return result
   397→
   398→    # Insert after any subjective items, before triage/workflow/issues
   399→    insert_at = 0
   400→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   401→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX):
   402→            insert_at = i + 1
   403→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID)
   404→    result.injected = True
   405→    return result
   406→
   407→
   408→@dataclass
   409→class CreatePlanSyncResult:
   410→    """What changed during a create-plan sync."""
   411→
   412→    injected: bool = False
   413→
   414→    @property
   415→    def changes(self) -> int:
   416→        return int(self.injected)
   417→
   418→
   419→def sync_create_plan_needed(
   420→    plan: PlanModel,
   421→    state: StateModel,
   422→    *,
   423→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   424→) -> CreatePlanSyncResult:
   425→    """Inject ``workflow::create-plan`` when reviews complete + objective backlog exists.
   426→
   427→    Only injects when:
   428→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   429→    - At least one objective issue exists
   430→    - ``workflow::create-plan`` is not already in the queue
   431→    - No triage stages are pending
   432→    """
   433→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   434→    result = CreatePlanSyncResult()
   435→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   436→
   437→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in order:
   438→        return result
   439→
   440→    # Don't inject if triage stages are pending
   441→    if any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS):
   442→        return result
   443→
   444→    # Check that no unscored dimensions remain
   445→    if policy is not None:
   446→        if policy.unscored_ids:
   447→            return result
   448→        has_objective = policy.has_objective_backlog
   449→    else:
   450→        unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   451→        if unscored:
   452→            return result
   453→        issues = state.get("issues", {})
   454→        has_objective = any(
   455→            f.get("status") == "open"
   456→            and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   457→            for f in issues.values()
   458→        )
   459→    if not has_objective:
   460→        return result
   461→
   462→    # Insert after any subjective/workflow items, at the end of the
   463→    # synthetic block (so create-plan comes after score-checkpoint).
   464→    insert_at = 0
   465→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   466→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) or fid.startswith(TRIAGE_PREFIX) or fid.startswith(WORKFLOW_PREFIX):
   467→            insert_at = i + 1
   468→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID)
   469→    result.injected = True
   470→    return result
   471→
   472→
   473→def compute_new_issue_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   474→    """Return the set of open review/concerns issue IDs added since last triage.
   475→
   476→    Returns an empty set when no prior triage has recorded ``triaged_ids``.
   477→    """
   478→    return stale_policy_mod.compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
   479→
   480→
   481→def is_triage_stale(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> bool:
   482→    """Side-effect-free check: is triage needed?
   483→
   484→    Returns True when genuinely *new* review issues appeared since the
   485→    last triage.  Triage stage IDs being in the queue alone is not
   486→    sufficient — the new issues that triggered injection may have been
   487→    resolved since then.
   488→
   489→    When issues are merely resolved (current IDs are a subset of
   490→    previously triaged IDs), triage is NOT stale — the user is working
   491→    through the plan.
   492→    """
   493→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   494→    return stale_policy_mod.is_triage_stale(plan, state, triage_ids=TRIAGE_IDS)
   495→
   496→
   497→@dataclass
   498→class ImportScoresSyncResult:
   499→    """What changed during an import-scores sync."""
   500→
   501→    injected: bool = False
   502→
   503→    @property
   504→    def changes(self) -> int:
   505→        return int(self.injected)
   506→
   507→
   508→def sync_import_scores_needed(
   509→    plan: PlanModel,
   510→    state: StateModel,
   511→    *,
   512→    assessment_mode: str | None = None,
   513→) -> ImportScoresSyncResult:
   514→    """Inject ``workflow::import-scores`` after issues-only import.
   515→
   516→    Only injects when:
   517→    - Assessment mode was ``issues_only`` (scores were skipped)
   518→    - ``workflow::import-scores`` is not already in the queue
   519→    - There are assessments in the payload that could be imported
   520→
   521→    Positioned after score-checkpoint, before create-plan.
   522→    """
   523→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   524→    result = ImportScoresSyncResult()
   525→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   526→
   527→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID in order:
   528→        return result
   529→
   530→    # Only inject when scores were skipped (issues-only mode)
   531→    if assessment_mode != "issues_only":
   532→        return result
   533→
   534→    # Insert after any subjective/workflow items
   535→    insert_at = 0
   536→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   537→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) or fid.startswith(WORKFLOW_PREFIX):
   538→            insert_at = i + 1
   539→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID)
   540→    result.injected = True
   541→    return result
   542→
   543→
   544→@dataclass
   545→class CommunicateScoreSyncResult:
   546→    """What changed during a communicate-score sync."""
   547→
   548→    injected: bool = False
   549→
   550→    @property
   551→    def changes(self) -> int:
   552→        return int(self.injected)
   553→
   554→
   555→def sync_communicate_score_needed(
   556→    plan: PlanModel,
   557→    state: StateModel,
   558→    *,
   559→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   560→    scores_just_imported: bool = False,
   561→) -> CommunicateScoreSyncResult:
   562→    """Inject ``workflow::communicate-score`` when scores should be shown.
   563→
   564→    Injects when either:
   565→    - All initial subjective reviews are complete (no unscored dimensions), OR
   566→    - Scores were just imported (trusted/attested/override)
   567→
   568→    And ``workflow::communicate-score`` is not already in the queue.
   569→    Positioned after subjective items but before triage/create-plan.
   570→    """
   571→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   572→    result = CommunicateScoreSyncResult()
   573→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   574→
   575→    # Also treat legacy score-checkpoint as already-present
   576→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in order or WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID in order:
   577→        return result
   578→
   579→    # Trigger 1: scores just imported
   580→    should_inject = scores_just_imported
   581→
   582→    # Trigger 2: all initial reviews complete (no unscored dimensions)
   583→    if not should_inject:
   584→        if policy is not None:
   585→            should_inject = not policy.unscored_ids
   586→        else:
   587→            should_inject = not current_unscored_ids(state)
   588→
   589→    if not should_inject:
   590→        return result
   591→
   592→    # Insert after any subjective items, before triage/workflow/issues
   593→    insert_at = 0
   594→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   595→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX):
   596→            insert_at = i + 1
   597→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID)
   598→    result.injected = True
   599→    return result
   600→
   601→
   602→__all__ = [
   603→    "SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX",
   604→    "TRIAGE_ID",
   605→    "TRIAGE_IDS",
   606→    "TRIAGE_PREFIX",
   607→    "TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS",
   608→    "SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES",
   609→    "WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID",
   610→    "WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID",
   611→    "WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID",
   612→    "WORKFLOW_PREFIX",
   613→    "WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID",
   614→    "CommunicateScoreSyncResult",
   615→    "CreatePlanSyncResult",
   616→    "ImportScoresSyncResult",
   617→    "ScoreCheckpointSyncResult",

1→"""Epic triage orchestrator and public API."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→import logging
     7→from dataclasses import dataclass
     8→from typing import Any
     9→
    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    11→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    12→
    13→from .epic_triage_apply import TriageMutationResult, apply_triage_to_plan
    14→from .epic_triage_parsing import (
    15→    ISSUE_ID_RE,
    16→    extract_issue_citations,
    17→    parse_triage_result,
    18→)
    19→from .epic_triage_prompt import (
    20→    _TRIAGE_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
    21→    ContradictionNote,
    22→    DismissedIssue,
    23→    TriageInput,
    24→    TriageResult,
    25→    build_triage_prompt,
    26→    collect_triage_input,
    27→)
    28→
    29→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    30→
    31→
    32→def last_real_review_timestamp(state: dict) -> str | None:
    33→    """ISO timestamp of most recent genuine review import (not manual override/scan reset)."""
    34→    REAL_MODES = {"holistic", "per_file", "trusted_internal", "attested_external"}
    35→    audit = state.get("assessment_import_audit", [])
    36→    if isinstance(audit, list):
    37→        for entry in reversed(audit):
    38→            if isinstance(entry, dict) and entry.get("mode") in REAL_MODES:
    39→                ts = entry.get("timestamp")
    40→                if ts:
    41→                    return str(ts)
    42→    holistic = (state.get("review_cache") or {}).get("holistic")
    43→    if isinstance(holistic, dict):
    44→        return holistic.get("reviewed_at")
    45→    return None
    46→
    47→def detect_recurring_patterns(
    48→    open_issues: dict[str, dict],
    49→    resolved_issues: dict[str, dict],
    50→) -> dict[str, dict]:
    51→    """Detect dimensions with both resolved AND current open issues.
    52→
    53→    Returns ``{dimension: {"open": [ids], "resolved": [ids]}}``.
    54→    A dimension with both resolved and open issues signals a potential
    55→    loop — similar issues recur after previous fixes.
    56→    """
    57→    def _dimension(f: dict) -> str:
    58→        detail = f.get("detail", {})
    59→        if isinstance(detail, dict):
    60→            return detail.get("dimension", "")
    61→        return ""
    62→
    63→    open_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    64→    for fid, f in open_issues.items():
    65→        dim = _dimension(f)
    66→        if dim:
    67→            open_by_dim.setdefault(dim, []).append(fid)
    68→
    69→    resolved_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    70→    for fid, f in resolved_issues.items():
    71→        dim = _dimension(f)
    72→        if dim:
    73→            resolved_by_dim.setdefault(dim, []).append(fid)
    74→
    75→    recurring: dict[str, dict] = {}
    76→    for dim in set(open_by_dim) & set(resolved_by_dim):
    77→        recurring[dim] = {
    78→            "open": open_by_dim[dim],
    79→            "resolved": resolved_by_dim[dim],
    80→        }
    81→    return recurring
    82→
    83→@dataclass
    84→class TriageDeps:
    85→    """Injectable dependencies for the triage engine."""
    86→
    87→    llm_call: Any = None  # Callable[[str, str], str] — (system, user) -> response
    88→
    89→def triage_epics(
    90→    plan: PlanModel,
    91→    state: StateModel,
    92→    *,
    93→    deps: TriageDeps | None = None,
    94→    dry_run: bool = False,
    95→    trigger: str = "manual",
    96→) -> TriageMutationResult:
    97→    """Run epic triage: collect input, call LLM, apply results.
    98→
    99→    If ``dry_run`` is True, collects input and builds prompt but does not
   100→    call the LLM or mutate the plan.
   101→
   102→    If ``deps.llm_call`` is None, returns a dry-run result with the prompt.
   103→    """
   104→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   105→    si = collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   106→
   107→    prompt = build_triage_prompt(si)
   108→    valid_ids = set(si.open_issues.keys())
   109→
   110→    if dry_run or deps is None or deps.llm_call is None:
   111→        result = TriageMutationResult(dry_run=True)
   112→        result.strategy_summary = f"[dry-run] Prompt built with {len(si.open_issues)} issues"
   113→        return result
   114→
   115→    # Call LLM
   116→    try:
   117→        raw_response = deps.llm_call(_TRIAGE_SYSTEM_PROMPT, prompt)
   118→        raw_json = json.loads(raw_response)
   119→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
   120→        logger.error("Epic triage LLM response parse error: %s", exc)
   121→        result = TriageMutationResult()
   122→        result.strategy_summary = f"Triage failed: {exc}"
   123→        return result
   124→
   125→    triage = parse_triage_result(raw_json, valid_ids)
   126→    return apply_triage_to_plan(plan, state, triage, trigger=trigger)
   127→
   128→__all__ = [
   129→    "ContradictionNote",
   130→    "DismissedIssue",
   131→    "ISSUE_ID_RE",
   132→    "TriageDeps",
   133→    "TriageInput",
   134→    "TriageMutationResult",
   135→    "TriageResult",
   136→    "apply_triage_to_plan",
   137→    "build_triage_prompt",
   138→    "collect_triage_input",
   139→    "detect_recurring_patterns",
   140→    "extract_issue_citations",
   141→    "last_real_review_timestamp",
   142→    "parse_triage_result",
   143→    "triage_epics",
   144→]
   145→

1→"""Prompt construction and data models for epic triage."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     9→    Cluster,
    10→    PlanModel,
    11→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    12→    triage_clusters,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    15→
    16→
    17→@dataclass
    18→class TriageInput:
    19→    """All data the LLM needs to produce/update epics."""
    20→
    21→    open_issues: dict[str, dict]       # id -> issue (review + concerns)
    22→    mechanical_issues: dict[str, dict]  # id -> issue (non-review, for context)
    23→    existing_epics: dict[str, Cluster]    # current triage-clusters to update
    24→    dimension_scores: dict[str, Any]      # for context
    25→    new_since_last: set[str]             # issue IDs new since last triage
    26→    resolved_since_last: set[str]        # issue IDs resolved since last
    27→    previously_dismissed: list[str]      # IDs dismissed in prior triage
    28→    triage_version: int                  # next version number
    29→    resolved_issues: dict[str, dict]   # full issue objects for resolved IDs
    30→    completed_clusters: list[dict]       # clusters completed since last triage
    31→
    32→@dataclass
    33→class DismissedIssue:
    34→    """A issue the LLM says doesn't make sense."""
    35→
    36→    issue_id: str
    37→    reason: str
    38→
    39→@dataclass
    40→class ContradictionNote:
    41→    """Record of a resolved contradiction."""
    42→
    43→    kept: str
    44→    dismissed: str
    45→    reason: str
    46→
    47→@dataclass
    48→class TriageResult:
    49→    """Parsed and validated LLM triage output."""
    50→
    51→    strategy_summary: str
    52→    epics: list[dict]
    53→    dismissed_issues: list[DismissedIssue] = field(default_factory=list)
    54→    contradiction_notes: list[ContradictionNote] = field(default_factory=list)
    55→    priority_rationale: str = ""
    56→
    57→def collect_triage_input(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> TriageInput:
    58→    """Gather all data needed for the triage LLM prompt."""
    59→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    60→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
    61→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    62→    epics = triage_clusters(plan)
    63→
    64→    open_review: dict[str, dict] = {}
    65→    open_mechanical: dict[str, dict] = {}
    66→    for fid, f in issues.items():
    67→        if f.get("status") != "open":
    68→            continue
    69→        if f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns"):
    70→            open_review[fid] = f
    71→        else:
    72→            open_mechanical[fid] = f
    73→
    74→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
    75→    current_review_ids = set(open_review.keys())
    76→    new_since = current_review_ids - triaged_ids
    77→    resolved_since = triaged_ids - current_review_ids
    78→    previously_dismissed = list(meta.get("dismissed_ids", []))
    79→    version = int(meta.get("version", 0)) + 1
    80→
    81→    # Resolved issue objects (for REFLECT stage)
    82→    resolved_issue_objs = {
    83→        fid: issues[fid] for fid in resolved_since if fid in issues
    84→    }
    85→
    86→    # Completed clusters since last triage completion
    87→    last_completed = meta.get("last_completed_at", "")
    88→    all_completed: list[dict] = plan.get("completed_clusters", [])
    89→    if last_completed:
    90→        recent_completed = [
    91→            c for c in all_completed
    92→            if c.get("completed_at", "") > last_completed
    93→        ]
    94→    else:
    95→        recent_completed = list(all_completed)
    96→
    97→    return TriageInput(
    98→        open_issues=open_review,
    99→        mechanical_issues=open_mechanical,
   100→        existing_epics=dict(epics),
   101→        dimension_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   102→        new_since_last=new_since,
   103→        resolved_since_last=resolved_since,
   104→        previously_dismissed=previously_dismissed,
   105→        triage_version=version,
   106→        resolved_issues=resolved_issue_objs,
   107→        completed_clusters=recent_completed,
   108→    )
   109→
   110→_TRIAGE_SYSTEM_PROMPT = """\
   111→You are maintaining the meta-plan for this codebase. Your goal is to produce
   112→a coherent, prioritized strategy to address ALL open review issues.
   113→
   114→Your plan should:
   115→- Cluster issues by ROOT CAUSE, not by dimension or detector
   116→- Give each cluster (epic) a clear thesis: one imperative sentence
   117→- Order epics by dependency: what must be done first for later work to make sense
   118→- Dismiss issues that don't make sense, are contradictory, or are false positives
   119→- Mark which epics are agent-safe (can be executed mechanically) vs need human judgment
   120→- Avoid creating work that contradicts other work in the plan
   121→- Be ambitious but realistic — aim to resolve all issues coherently
   122→
   123→Available directions for epics: delete, merge, flatten, enforce, simplify, decompose, extract, inline.
   124→
   125→Available plan tools (the agent executing your plan has access to these):
   126→- `desloppify plan queue` — view all items in priority order
   127→- `desloppify plan focus epic/<name>` — focus the queue on one epic
   128→- `desloppify plan skip <id> --permanent --note "why" --attest "..."` — permanently dismiss
   129→- `desloppify plan skip <id> --note "revisit later"` — temporarily defer
   130→- `desloppify plan resolve <id> --note "what I did" --attest "..."` — mark resolved
   131→- `desloppify plan reorder <id> top|bottom|before|after <target>` — reorder
   132→- `desloppify plan cluster show <name>` — inspect a cluster
   133→- `desloppify scan` — re-scan after making changes to verify progress
   134→- `desloppify show review --status open` — see all open review issues
   135→
   136→Your output defines the ENTIRE work plan. Issues not assigned to any epic
   137→will remain in the queue as individual items. Dismissed issues will be
   138→removed from the queue with your stated reason.
   139→
   140→Respond with a single JSON object matching this schema:
   141→{
   142→  "strategy_summary": "2-4 sentence narrative: what the meta-plan says, top priorities, current state",
   143→  "epics": [
   144→    {
   145→      "name": "slug-name",
   146→      "thesis": "imperative one-liner",
   147→      "direction": "delete|merge|flatten|enforce|simplify|decompose|extract|inline",
   148→      "root_cause": "why this cluster exists",
   149→      "issue_ids": ["id1", "id2"],
   150→      "dismissed": ["id3"],
   151→      "agent_safe": true,
   152→      "dependency_order": 1,
   153→      "action_steps": ["step 1", "step 2"],
   154→      "status": "pending"
   155→    }
   156→  ],
   157→  "dismissed_issues": [
   158→    {"issue_id": "id", "reason": "why this issue doesn't make sense"}
   159→  ],
   160→  "contradiction_notes": [
   161→    {"kept": "issue_id", "dismissed": "issue_id", "reason": "why"}
   162→  ],
   163→  "priority_rationale": "why the dependency_order is what it is"
   164→}
   165→"""
   166→
   167→def build_triage_prompt(si: TriageInput) -> str:
   168→    """Build the user-facing prompt content with all issue data."""
   169→    parts: list[str] = []
   170→
   171→    # Section: existing epics
   172→    if si.existing_epics:
   173→        parts.append("## Existing Epics (update these, don't recreate)")
   174→        for name, epic in sorted(si.existing_epics.items()):
   175→            status = epic.get("status", "pending")
   176→            thesis = epic.get("thesis", "")
   177→            direction = epic.get("direction", "")
   178→            fids = epic.get("issue_ids", [])
   179→            parts.append(
   180→                f"- {name} [{status}] ({direction}): {thesis}"
   181→                f"\n  Issues: {', '.join(fids[:10])}"
   182→                f"{'...' if len(fids) > 10 else ''}"
   183→            )
   184→        parts.append("")
   185→
   186→    # Section: what changed
   187→    if si.new_since_last:
   188→        parts.append(f"## New issues since last triage ({len(si.new_since_last)})")
   189→        for fid in sorted(si.new_since_last):
   190→            f = si.open_issues.get(fid, {})
   191→            parts.append(f"- {fid}: {f.get('summary', '(no summary)')}")
   192→        parts.append("")
   193→
   194→    if si.resolved_since_last:
   195→        parts.append(f"## Resolved since last triage ({len(si.resolved_since_last)})")
   196→        for fid in sorted(si.resolved_since_last):
   197→            parts.append(f"- {fid}")
   198→        parts.append("")
   199→
   200→    # Section: all open review issues
   201→    parts.append(f"## All open review issues ({len(si.open_issues)})")
   202→    for fid, f in sorted(si.open_issues.items()):
   203→        detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   204→        suggestion = detail.get("suggestion", "")
   205→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
   206→        confidence = f.get("confidence", "medium")
   207→        file_path = f.get("file", "")
   208→        summary = f.get("summary", "")
   209→        parts.append(f"- [{confidence}] {fid}")
   210→        parts.append(f"  File: {file_path}")
   211→        if dimension:
   212→            parts.append(f"  Dimension: {dimension}")
   213→        parts.append(f"  Summary: {summary}")
   214→        if suggestion:
   215→            parts.append(f"  Suggestion: {suggestion}")
   216→    parts.append("")
   217→
   218→    # Section: dimension scores for context
   219→    if si.dimension_scores:
   220→        parts.append("## Dimension scores (context)")
   221→        for name, data in sorted(si.dimension_scores.items()):
   222→            if isinstance(data, dict):
   223→                score = data.get("score", "?")
   224→                strict = data.get("strict", score)
   225→                issues = data.get("failing", 0)
   226→                parts.append(f"- {name}: {score}% (strict: {strict}%, {issues} issues)")
   227→        parts.append("")
   228→
   229→    # Section: previously dismissed
   230→    if si.previously_dismissed:
   231→        parts.append(f"## Previously dismissed ({len(si.previously_dismissed)})")
   232→        parts.append("Maintain unless contradicted by new evidence.")
   233→        for fid in si.previously_dismissed:
   234→            parts.append(f"- {fid}")
   235→        parts.append("")
   236→
   237→    return "\n".join(parts)
   238→
   239→__all__ = [
   240→    "_TRIAGE_SYSTEM_PROMPT",
   241→    "ContradictionNote",
   242→    "DismissedIssue",
   243→    "TriageInput",
   244→    "TriageResult",
   245→    "build_triage_prompt",
   246→    "collect_triage_input",
   247→]
   248→
```

1→"""Plan mutation helpers for epic triage."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     8→    EPIC_PREFIX,
     9→    Cluster,
    10→    PlanModel,
    11→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import review_issue_snapshot_hash
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    15→
    16→from .epic_triage_prompt import TriageResult
    17→
    18→
    19→@dataclass
    20→class TriageMutationResult:
    21→    """What changed when triage was applied to the plan."""
    22→
    23→    epics_created: int = 0
    24→    epics_updated: int = 0
    25→    epics_completed: int = 0
    26→    issues_dismissed: int = 0
    27→    issues_reassigned: int = 0
    28→    strategy_summary: str = ""
    29→    triage_version: int = 0
    30→    dry_run: bool = False
    31→
    32→def apply_triage_to_plan(
    33→    plan: PlanModel,
    34→    state: StateModel,
    35→    triage: TriageResult,
    36→    *,
    37→    trigger: str = "manual",
    38→) -> TriageMutationResult:
    39→    """Apply parsed triage result to the living plan.
    40→
    41→    1. Creates/updates triage-clusters in plan["clusters"]
    42→    2. Marks dismissed issues as triaged_out skips
    43→    3. Reorders queue_order to group epic members by dependency_order
    44→    4. Updates epic_triage_meta with snapshot hash
    45→    """
    46→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    47→    now = utc_now()
    48→    result = TriageMutationResult()
    49→    result.strategy_summary = triage.strategy_summary
    50→
    51→    clusters = plan["clusters"]
    52→    skipped: dict = plan["skipped"]
    53→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    54→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    55→    version = int(meta.get("version", 0)) + 1
    56→    result.triage_version = version
    57→
    58→    # --- Update/create triage-clusters ------------------------------------
    59→    for epic_data in sorted(triage.epics, key=lambda e: e.get("dependency_order", 999)):
    60→        raw_name = epic_data["name"]
    61→        epic_name = f"{EPIC_PREFIX}{raw_name}" if not raw_name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX) else raw_name
    62→
    63→        existing = clusters.get(epic_name)
    64→        if existing and existing.get("thesis"):
    65→            # Update existing triage-cluster
    66→            existing["thesis"] = epic_data["thesis"]
    67→            existing["direction"] = epic_data["direction"]
    68→            existing["root_cause"] = epic_data.get("root_cause", "")
    69→            existing["issue_ids"] = epic_data["issue_ids"]
    70→            existing["dismissed"] = epic_data.get("dismissed", [])
    71→            existing["agent_safe"] = epic_data.get("agent_safe", False)
    72→            existing["dependency_order"] = epic_data["dependency_order"]
    73→            existing["action_steps"] = epic_data.get("action_steps", [])
    74→            existing["updated_at"] = now
    75→            existing["triage_version"] = version
    76→            existing["description"] = epic_data["thesis"]
    77→            # Don't overwrite in_progress status from agent
    78→            if existing.get("status") != "in_progress":
    79→                existing["status"] = epic_data.get("status", "pending")
    80→            result.epics_updated += 1
    81→        else:
    82→            # Create new triage-cluster
    83→            cluster: Cluster = {
    84→                "name": epic_name,
    85→                "description": epic_data["thesis"],
    86→                "issue_ids": epic_data["issue_ids"],
    87→                "auto": True,
    88→                "cluster_key": f"epic::{epic_name}",
    89→                "action": f"desloppify plan focus {epic_name}",
    90→                "user_modified": False,
    91→                "created_at": now,
    92→                "updated_at": now,
    93→                # Epic fields
    94→                "thesis": epic_data["thesis"],
    95→                "direction": epic_data["direction"],
    96→                "root_cause": epic_data.get("root_cause", ""),
    97→                "supersedes": [],
    98→                "dismissed": epic_data.get("dismissed", []),
    99→                "agent_safe": epic_data.get("agent_safe", False),
   100→                "dependency_order": epic_data["dependency_order"],
   101→                "action_steps": epic_data.get("action_steps", []),
   102→                "source_clusters": [],
   103→                "status": epic_data.get("status", "pending"),
   104→                "triage_version": version,
   105→            }
   106→            clusters[epic_name] = cluster
   107→            result.epics_created += 1
   108→
   109→    # --- Dismiss issues ---------------------------------------------------
   110→    dismissed_ids: list[str] = []
   111→    for df in triage.dismissed_issues:
   112→        fid = df.issue_id
   113→        dismissed_ids.append(fid)
   114→        # Remove from queue, add to skipped as triaged_out
   115→        if fid in order:
   116→            order.remove(fid)
   117→        skipped[fid] = {
   118→            "issue_id": fid,
   119→            "kind": "triaged_out",
   120→            "reason": df.reason,
   121→            "note": f"Dismissed by epic triage v{version}",
   122→            "attestation": None,
   123→            "created_at": now,
   124→            "review_after": None,
   125→            "skipped_at_scan": int(state.get("scan_count", 0)),
   126→        }
   127→        result.issues_dismissed += 1
   128→
   129→    # Also handle per-epic dismissed lists
   130→    for epic_data in triage.epics:
   131→        for fid in epic_data.get("dismissed", []):
   132→            if fid not in dismissed_ids and fid in order:
   133→                order.remove(fid)
   134→                dismissed_ids.append(fid)
   135→                skipped[fid] = {
   136→                    "issue_id": fid,
   137→                    "kind": "triaged_out",
   138→                    "reason": f"Dismissed by epic triage v{version}",
   139→                    "note": None,
   140→                    "attestation": None,
   141→                    "created_at": now,
   142→                    "review_after": None,
   143→                    "skipped_at_scan": int(state.get("scan_count", 0)),
   144→                }
   145→                result.issues_dismissed += 1
   146→
   147→    # --- Reorder queue: epic issues grouped by dependency_order -----------
   148→    epic_issue_ids: set[str] = set()
   149→    epic_ordered_ids: list[str] = []
   150→    for epic_data in sorted(triage.epics, key=lambda e: e.get("dependency_order", 999)):
   151→        for fid in epic_data["issue_ids"]:
   152→            if fid not in epic_issue_ids and fid not in dismissed_ids:
   153→                epic_issue_ids.add(fid)
   154→                epic_ordered_ids.append(fid)
   155→
   156→    # Rebuild order: epic items first (by dependency), then non-epic items in original order
   157→    non_epic_items = [fid for fid in order if fid not in epic_issue_ids]
   158→    # Insert epic items at the front.
   159→    new_order: list[str] = []
   160→    new_order.extend(epic_ordered_ids)
   161→    new_order.extend(non_epic_items)
   162→    order.clear()
   163→    order.extend(new_order)
   164→
   165→    # --- Update triage meta -----------------------------------------------
   166→    current_hash = review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   167→    open_review_ids = sorted(
   168→        fid for fid, f in state.get("issues", {}).items()
   169→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   170→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   171→    )
   172→
   173→    plan["epic_triage_meta"] = {
   174→        "triaged_ids": open_review_ids,
   175→        "last_run": now,
   176→        "version": version,
   177→        "dismissed_ids": dismissed_ids,
   178→        "issue_snapshot_hash": current_hash,
   179→        "strategy_summary": triage.strategy_summary,
   180→        "trigger": trigger,
   181→    }
   182→    plan["updated"] = now
   183→
   184→    return result
   185→
   186→__all__ = ["TriageMutationResult", "apply_triage_to_plan"]
   187→
```

    43→def cmd_plan_output(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    44→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state."""
    45→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    46→    state = runtime.state
    47→
    48→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    49→        return
    50→
    51→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(runtime.config)
    52→    if config_warning:
    53→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    54→
    55→    plan_md = planning_mod.generate_plan_md(state)
    56→    next_command = "desloppify next --count 20"
    57→
    58→    output = getattr(args, "output", None)
    59→    if output:
    60→        try:
    61→            safe_write_text(output, plan_md)
    62→            print(colorize(f"Plan written to {output}", "green"))
    63→            print_agent_plan(
    64→                ["Inspect and execute the generated plan."],
    65→                next_command=next_command,
    66→            )
    67→        except OSError as e:
    68→            warn_best_effort(f"Could not write plan to {output}: {e}")
    69→    else:
    70→        print(plan_md)
    71→        print()
    72→        print_agent_plan(
    73→            ["Start from the top-ranked action in this plan."],
    74→            next_command=next_command,
    75→        )
    76→
    77→
    78→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    79→    """Generate the prioritized markdown plan (existing behavior)."""
    80→    cmd_plan_output(args)
    81→

    83→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    84→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    85→    plan = load_plan()
    86→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    87→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    88→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    89→    kind_counts = {
    90→        kind: sum(1 for entry in skipped.values() if entry.get("kind") == kind)
    91→        for kind in USER_SKIP_KINDS
    92→    }
    93→    temp_count = kind_counts["temporary"]
    94→    perm_count = kind_counts["permanent"]
    95→    fp_count = kind_counts["false_positive"]
    96→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    97→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    98→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    99→
   100→    described, noted = annotation_counts(plan)
   101→
   102→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
   103→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
   104→    print(f"  Queue:            {ordered} items prioritized")
   105→    if total_skipped:
   106→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
   107→    else:
   108→        print("  Skipped:          0")
   109→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
   110→    if clusters:
   111→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   112→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   113→            member_count = len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   114→            marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
   115→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   116→            print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
   117→    if described or noted:
   118→        print(f"  Annotations:      {described} described, {noted} noted")
   119→    if active:
   120→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
   121→    if superseded:
   122→        print(f"  Disappeared:      {superseded} (resolved or removed since last scan)")
   123→
   124→    # Commit tracking summary
   125→    _cfg = load_config()
   126→    if _cfg.get("commit_tracking_enabled", True):
   127→        ct = commit_tracking_summary(plan)
   128→        if ct["total"] > 0:
   129→            pr_num = _cfg.get("commit_pr", 0)
   130→            pr_str = f"  PR: #{pr_num}" if pr_num else ""
   131→            print(
   132→                f"  Commit tracking:  {ct['uncommitted']} uncommitted, "
   133→                f"{ct['committed']} committed ({ct['total']} issues){pr_str}"
   134→            )
   135→
   136→
   137→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   138→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
   139→    plan = load_plan()
   140→    queue_len = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   141→    cluster_count = len(plan.get("clusters", {}))
   142→    reset_plan(plan)
   143→    append_log_entry(
   144→        plan, "reset", actor="user",
   145→        detail={"previous_queue_size": queue_len, "previous_cluster_count": cluster_count},
   146→    )
   147→    save_plan(plan)
   148→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
   149→
   150→
   151→_PLAN_ACTION_HANDLERS = {
   152→    "show": _cmd_plan_show,
   153→    "queue": cmd_plan_queue,
   154→    "reset": _cmd_plan_reset,
   155→    "reorder": cmd_plan_reorder,
   156→    "describe": cmd_plan_describe,
   157→    "resolve": cmd_plan_resolve,
   158→    "note": cmd_plan_note,
   159→    "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   160→    "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   161→    "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   162→    "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   163→    "cluster": cmd_cluster_dispatch,
   164→    "triage": cmd_plan_triage,
   165→    "commit-log": cmd_commit_log_dispatch,
   166→}
   167→
   168→
   169→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   170→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
   171→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
   172→    if plan_action is None:
   173→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
   174→        return
   175→
   176→    handler = _PLAN_ACTION_HANDLERS.get(plan_action)
   177→    if handler is None:
   178→        print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
   179→        return
   180→    handler(args)
   181→
   182→__all__ = ["cmd_plan", "cmd_plan_output"]

1→"""Handler for ``plan triage`` subcommand."""
     2→

    10→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage import display as _display_mod
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage import helpers as _helpers_mod
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage import _stage_completion_commands as _completion_mod
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage import _stage_flow_commands as _flow_mod
    14→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage import _stage_validation as _validation_mod
    15→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage_playbook import TRIAGE_CMD_OBSERVE
    16→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    17→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    18→    append_log_entry,
    19→    build_triage_prompt,
    20→    collect_triage_input,
    21→    detect_recurring_patterns,
    22→    extract_issue_citations,
    23→    load_plan,
    24→    save_plan,
    25→)
    26→
    27→_MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN = _confirmations_mod._MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN
    28→_validate_attestation = _confirmations_mod._validate_attestation
    29→_triage_coverage = _helpers_mod._triage_coverage
    30→
    31→
    32→_TRIAGE_SUBMODULES = (
    33→    _helpers_mod, _display_mod, _confirmations_mod,
    34→    _flow_mod, _completion_mod, _validation_mod,
    35→)
    36→
    37→
    38→def _sync_triage_module_bindings() -> None:
    39→    """Propagate monkeypatch-friendly bindings into split triage modules.
    40→
    41→    Each submodule imports ``command_runtime``, ``load_plan``, etc. at the
    42→    top level.  Tests monkeypatch those names on *this* handler module; this
    43→    function copies the (potentially patched) references into every submodule
    44→    that already has the name, so mocks propagate automatically.
    45→
    46→    Adding a new submodule: append it to ``_TRIAGE_SUBMODULES``.
    47→    Adding a new patchable function: add it to the ``bindings`` dict below
    48→    *and* import it at the top of this file — submodules that import the same
    49→    name will pick it up automatically via ``hasattr``.
    50→    """
    51→    bindings = {
    52→        "command_runtime": command_runtime,
    53→        "save_plan": save_plan,
    54→        "load_plan": load_plan,
    55→        "collect_triage_input": collect_triage_input,
    56→        "detect_recurring_patterns": detect_recurring_patterns,
    57→        "append_log_entry": append_log_entry,
    58→        "extract_issue_citations": extract_issue_citations,
    59→    }
    60→    for mod in _TRIAGE_SUBMODULES:
    61→        for name, fn in bindings.items():
    62→            if hasattr(mod, name):
    63→                setattr(mod, name, fn)
    64→
    65→
    66→def _cmd_triage_start(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    67→    """Manually inject triage stage IDs into the queue and clear prior stages."""
    68→    _sync_triage_module_bindings()
    69→    plan = load_plan()
    70→
    71→    if _helpers_mod._has_triage_in_queue(plan):
    72→        print(colorize("  Planning mode stages are already in the queue.", "yellow"))
    73→        meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    74→        stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
    75→        if stages:
    76→            print(
    77→                colorize(
    78→                    f"  {len(stages)} stage(s) in progress — clearing to restart.", "yellow"
    79→                )
    80→            )
    81→            meta["triage_stages"] = {}
    82→            _helpers_mod._inject_triage_stages(plan)
    83→            save_plan(plan)
    84→            append_log_entry(
    85→                plan,
    86→                "triage_start",
    87→                actor="user",
    88→                detail={"action": "restart", "cleared_stages": list(stages.keys())},
    89→            )
    90→            save_plan(plan)
    91→            print(colorize("  Stages cleared. Begin with observe:", "green"))
    92→        else:
    93→            print(colorize("  Begin with observe:", "green"))
    94→        print(colorize(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_OBSERVE}", "dim"))
    95→        return
    96→
    97→    _helpers_mod._inject_triage_stages(plan)
    98→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})
    99→    meta["triage_stages"] = {}
   100→    save_plan(plan)
   101→
   102→    append_log_entry(plan, "triage_start", actor="user", detail={"action": "start"})
   103→    save_plan(plan)
   104→
   105→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   106→    si = collect_triage_input(plan, runtime.state)
   107→    print(colorize("  Planning mode started (4 stages queued).", "green"))
   108→    print(f"  Open review issues: {len(si.open_issues)}")
   109→    print(colorize("  Begin with observe:", "dim"))
   110→    print(colorize(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_OBSERVE}", "dim"))
   111→
   112→
   113→def cmd_plan_triage(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   114→    """Run epic triage: staged workflow OBSERVE → REFLECT → ORGANIZE → COMMIT."""
   115→    _sync_triage_module_bindings()
   116→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   117→    state = runtime.state
   118→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   119→        return
   120→
   121→    if getattr(args, "start", False):
   122→        _cmd_triage_start(args)
   123→        return
   124→    if getattr(args, "confirm", None):
   125→        _confirmations_mod._cmd_confirm_stage(args)
   126→        return
   127→    if getattr(args, "complete", False):
   128→        _completion_mod._cmd_triage_complete(args)
   129→        return
   130→    if getattr(args, "confirm_existing", False):
   131→        _completion_mod._cmd_confirm_existing(args)
   132→        return
   133→
   134→    stage = getattr(args, "stage", None)
   135→    if stage == "observe":
   136→        _flow_mod._cmd_stage_observe(args)
   137→        return
   138→    if stage == "reflect":
   139→        _flow_mod._cmd_stage_reflect(args)
   140→        return
   141→    if stage == "organize":
   142→        _flow_mod._cmd_stage_organize(args)
   143→        return
   144→
   145→    if getattr(args, "dry_run", False):
   146→        plan = load_plan()
   147→        si = collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   148→        prompt = build_triage_prompt(si)
   149→        print(colorize("  Epic triage — dry run", "bold"))
   150→        print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   151→        print(f"  Open review issues: {len(si.open_issues)}")
   152→        print(f"  Existing epics: {len(si.existing_epics)}")
   153→        print(f"  New since last: {len(si.new_since_last)}")
   154→        print(f"  Resolved since last: {len(si.resolved_since_last)}")
   155→        print(colorize("\n  Prompt that would be sent to LLM:", "dim"))
   156→        print()
   157→        print(prompt)
   158→        return
   159→
   160→    _display_mod._cmd_triage_dashboard(args)
   161→
   162→__all__ = [
   163→    "_MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN",
   164→    "_triage_coverage",
   165→    "_validate_attestation",
   166→    "cmd_plan_triage",
   167→]
   168→

    40→def _print_stage_progress(stages: dict, plan: dict | None = None) -> None:
    41→    """Print the 4-stage progress indicator."""
    42→    print(colorize("  Stages:", "dim"))
    43→    for stage_name, label in TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS:
    44→        if stage_name in stages:
    45→            if stages[stage_name].get("confirmed_at"):
    46→                print(colorize(f"    \u2713 {label} (confirmed)", "green"))
    47→            else:
    48→                print(colorize(f"    \u2713 {label} (needs confirm)", "yellow"))
    49→        elif TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES[stage_name].issubset(stages):
    50→            print(colorize(f"    \u2192 {label} (current)", "yellow"))
    51→        else:
    52→            print(colorize(f"    \u25cb {label}", "dim"))
    53→
    54→    # Show enrichment gaps when in the organize stage
    55→    if plan and "reflect" in stages and "organize" not in stages:
    56→        gaps = _unenriched_clusters(plan)
    57→        manual = _manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
    58→        if not manual:
    59→            print(colorize("\n    No manual clusters yet. Create clusters and enrich them.", "yellow"))
    60→        elif gaps:
    61→            print(colorize(f"\n    {len(gaps)} cluster(s) need enrichment:", "yellow"))
    62→            for name, missing in gaps:
    63→                print(colorize(f"      {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
    64→            print(colorize(
    65→                f"      Fix: {TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ENRICH_COMPACT}",
    66→                "dim",
    67→            ))
    68→        else:
    69→            print(colorize(f"\n    All {len(manual)} manual cluster(s) enriched.", "green"))
    70→
    71→def _print_progress(plan: dict, open_issues: dict) -> None:
    72→    """Show cluster state and unclustered issues."""
    73→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    74→    # Only show clusters that actually have issues (hide empty/stale ones)
    75→    active_clusters = {
    76→        name: c for name, c in clusters.items()
    77→        if c.get("issue_ids")
    78→    }
    79→    if active_clusters:
    80→        print(colorize("\n  Current clusters:", "cyan"))
    81→        for name, cluster in active_clusters.items():
    82→            count = len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
    83→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    84→            steps = cluster.get("action_steps", [])
    85→            auto = cluster.get("auto", False)
    86→            tags: list[str] = []
    87→            if auto:
    88→                tags.append("auto")
    89→            if desc:
    90→                tags.append("desc")
    91→            else:
    92→                tags.append("no desc")
    93→            if steps:
    94→                tags.append(f"{len(steps)} steps")
    95→            else:
    96→                if not auto:
    97→                    tags.append("no steps")
    98→            tag_str = f" [{', '.join(tags)}]"
    99→            desc_str = f" \u2014 {desc}" if desc else ""
   100→            print(f"    {name}: {count} items{tag_str}{desc_str}")
   101→
   102→    all_clustered: set[str] = set()
   103→    for c in clusters.values():
   104→        all_clustered.update(c.get("issue_ids", []))
   105→    unclustered = [fid for fid in open_issues if fid not in all_clustered]
   106→    if unclustered:
   107→        print(colorize(f"\n  {len(unclustered)} issues not yet in a cluster:", "yellow"))
   108→        for fid in unclustered[:10]:
   109→            f = open_issues[fid]
   110→            dim = (f.get("detail", {}) or {}).get("dimension", "") if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else ""
   111→            short = short_issue_id(fid)
   112→            print(f"    [{short}] [{dim}] {f.get('summary', '')}")
   113→        if len(unclustered) > 10:
   114→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(unclustered) - 10} more", "dim"))
   115→    elif open_issues:
   116→        organized, total, _ = _triage_coverage(plan, open_review_ids=set(open_issues.keys()))
   117→        print(colorize(f"\n  All {organized}/{total} issues are in clusters.", "green"))

   119→def _print_reflect_result(
   120→    *,
   121→    issue_count: int,
   122→    recurring_dims: list[str],
   123→    recurring: dict,
   124→    report: str,
   125→    is_reuse: bool,
   126→    cleared: list,
   127→    stages: dict,
   128→) -> None:
   129→    """Print the reflect stage output including briefing box and next steps."""
   130→    print(colorize(
   131→        f"  Reflect stage recorded: {issue_count} issues, "
   132→        f"{len(recurring_dims)} recurring dimension(s).",
   133→        "green",
   134→    ))
   135→    if is_reuse:
   136→        print(colorize("  Reflect data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   137→        if cleared:
   138→            _print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   139→    else:
   140→        print(colorize("  Now confirm your strategy.", "yellow"))
   141→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan triage --confirm reflect", "dim"))
   142→    if recurring_dims:
   143→        for dim in recurring_dims:
   144→            info = recurring[dim]
   145→            print(colorize(
   146→                f"    {dim}: {len(info['resolved'])} resolved, {len(info['open'])} still open",
   147→                "dim",
   148→            ))
   149→
   150→    print()
   151→    print(colorize("  \u250c\u2500 Strategic briefing (share with user before organizing) \u2500\u2510", "cyan"))
   152→    for line in report.strip().splitlines():
   153→        print(colorize(f"  \u2502 {line}", "cyan"))
   154→    print(colorize("  \u2514" + "\u2500" * 57 + "\u2518", "cyan"))
   155→    print()
   156→    print(colorize(
   157→        "  IMPORTANT: Present your holistic strategy to the user. Explain:",

   160→    print(colorize(
