> AGENT

Here is a comprehensive summary of the full plan/triage process in desloppify.

---

## Plan Architecture Overview

The plan system manages a prioritized work queue stored in `.desloppify/plan-{lang}.json`. The queue (`queue_order`) is a list of item IDs that can be real issue IDs, or synthetic IDs representing workflow checkpoints. The plan also tracks clusters (groups of related issues), skipped items, commit records, and triage metadata.

---

## Queue Item Types

The queue contains four distinct ID namespaces:

- **Real issue IDs** — actual findings from scans (e.g., `review::abc123`, `concerns::def456`)
- **`subjective::<slug>`** — subjective dimension reviews (architecture, naming conventions, etc.) requiring human judgment
- **`triage::<stage>`** — triage workflow checkpoints (`triage::observe`, `triage::reflect`, `triage::organize`, `triage::commit`)
- **`workflow::<id>`** — onboarding workflow steps (`workflow::score-checkpoint` / `workflow::communicate-score`, `workflow::create-plan`, `workflow::import-scores`)

---

## Lifecycle Phases

### Phase 1: Onboarding (First-Run)

When desloppify is first run on a codebase:

1. **Unscored subjective dimensions** are injected at the front of the queue unconditionally (`sync_unscored_dimensions`). These are dimensions like "naming conventions" or "architecture" that need a human score before any objective issue fixing makes sense. Each appears as `subjective::<dim-slug>`.

2. Once all unscored dimensions are complete (all have scores), **`workflow::communicate-score`** (formerly `workflow::score-checkpoint`) is injected — a prompt to review the current strict score before proceeding.

3. If there is an objective backlog and triage is not pending, **`workflow::create-plan`** is injected — a prompt to run `plan triage` and establish the working plan.

### Phase 2: Triage (When Review Issues Appear)

Triage is triggered when `sync_triage_needed` detects that new open review/concerns issues exist that weren't present in the last triage snapshot. It injects the 4 triage stage IDs at the front of the queue.

A banner is shown: `TRIAGE MODE — review issues need analysis before fixing. Run: desloppify plan triage`

### Phase 3: Normal Execution

Once triage is complete, real issue IDs and stale subjective dimensions populate the queue. The user works through them via `desloppify next` and `desloppify plan resolve`.

**Stale subjective dimensions** (`sync_stale_dimensions`) are re-injected:
- At the back of the queue when no objective issues remain (mid-cycle)
- At the front after a full cycle completes (post-cycle priority)

---

## The 4-Stage Triage Workflow

This is the core of the plan's triage process. It is a structured analysis flow the agent (or user) must complete when new review issues appear.

### Stage 1: OBSERVE
**Label:** "Analyse issues & spot contradictions"
**Command:** `desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "analysis of themes and root causes..."`

The agent reads all open review issues (grouped by dimension for display) and writes a qualitative analysis report. The goal is to identify:
- Themes and root causes across issues
- Contradictions: issues in the same dimension recommending opposite changes (these must be resolved before clustering)

**Guardrails:**
- `--report` is required
- Minimum 100 characters (50 if 3 or fewer issues)
- Must not just list IDs — must describe actual observations

**Confirmation:** `desloppify plan triage --confirm observe --attestation "I have thoroughly reviewed..."`
- Attestation must be at least 80 characters
- Attestation must reference at least one dimension name from the issues

**What's shown to the user:** Issues grouped by dimension with their summaries and suggestions. A breakdown of issue counts per dimension.

---

### Stage 2: REFLECT
**Label:** "Form strategy & present to user"
**Command:** `desloppify plan triage --stage reflect --report "comparison against completed work..."`

The agent compares current issues against the history of what has been resolved before, and forms a holistic strategy. The dashboard shows:
- Previously completed clusters (with theses and action steps)
- Resolved issues since last triage (with dimension/status)
- Recurring pattern detection: dimensions where issues have both been resolved AND are open again (potential fix loops)

**Guardrails:**
- Requires observe stage to be confirmed first
- Minimum 100 character report
- If recurring patterns are detected, the report MUST mention at least one recurring dimension name (enforced by `_validate_recurring_dimension_mentions`)
- Can auto-confirm observe inline by passing `--attestation`

**After recording:** The agent is instructed to present the strategic briefing to the user before creating any clusters. The output explicitly says:
> IMPORTANT: Present your holistic strategy to the user. Explain:
> - What themes and root causes you see
> - What contradictions you found and which direction you'll take
> - What you'll prioritize, what you'll defer, the overall arc of work
> Wait for their input before creating clusters.

**Confirmation:** `desloppify plan triage --confirm reflect --attestation "My strategy accounts for..."`
- Attestation must reference at least one dimension or cluster name

---

### Stage 3: ORGANIZE
**Label:** "Defer contradictions, cluster, & prioritize"
**Command:** `desloppify plan triage --stage organize --report "summary of organization and priorities..."`

The agent performs the actual structural work:
1. Defer contradictory issues: `desloppify plan skip <hash>`
2. Create clusters: `desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "..."`
3. Add issues to clusters: `desloppify plan cluster add <name> <issue-patterns>`
4. Enrich each cluster with action steps: `desloppify plan cluster update <name> --steps "step 1" "step 2" ...`
5. Record the organize stage with a priority summary

**Guardrails (enforced at record time and completion time):**
- `reflect` must be confirmed first (can auto-confirm via `--attestation`)
- At least one manual cluster with issues must exist (auto-clusters don't count)
- All manual clusters must be "enriched" (have both `description` and `action_steps`)
- Report minimum 100 characters explaining priorities and ordering

**What "enriched" means:** Each cluster must have a `description` field and at least one `action_steps` entry. The `_unenriched_clusters` function detects gaps.

**After recording:** The agent is instructed to present the prioritized organization to the user — which cluster comes first, second, third, what depends on what, and why.

**Confirmation:** `desloppify plan triage --confirm organize --attestation "This plan is correct..."`
- Shows a full plan summary (clusters + queue order + coverage %)
- Shows all plan operations logged since reflect
- Attestation must reference at least one cluster name

---

### Stage 4: COMMIT (Complete)
**Label:** "Write strategy & confirm"
**Command:** `desloppify plan triage --complete --strategy "execution plan with priorities and verification..."`

This is the completion step that seals the triage cycle.

**Guardrails:**
- Organize must be confirmed (can auto-confirm via `--attestation`)
- Must have at least some issues organized into clusters
- `--strategy` is required: either `"same"` (keep existing) or a new 200+ character execution plan describing priorities, ordering, and verification approach
- Re-validates cluster enrichment at completion time (prevents bypassing via direct file editing)

**What happens at completion:**
- All 4 triage stage IDs are purged from the queue
- The current review issue set is recorded as `triaged_ids` in `epic_triage_meta`
- The snapshot hash is updated (so future scans can detect new issues)
- Stage reports are archived to `last_triage` for future reference
- `epic_triage_meta.strategy_summary` is updated
- Output: `Triage complete: N/M issues in K cluster(s). Run 'desloppify next' to start implementation.`

---

## Fast-Track: Confirm Existing
**Command:** `desloppify plan triage --confirm-existing --note "..." --strategy "..." --confirmed "I validate..."`

Available only after at least one completed triage. Used when new issues appear but the existing plan structure is still valid.

**Two paths:**
- **Additions only** (new issues, no resolutions): lighter ceremony — only needs `--note` (citing at least one new issue ID), and defaults `--strategy "same"`
- **Mixed changes**: requires observe and reflect stages first

**Guardrails:** requires existing enriched clusters, note must be 100+ chars, note must cite at least one new issue ID, `--confirmed` text must be 80+ chars.

---

## Plan Reconciliation (Post-Scan)

After every scan, `reconcile_plan_after_scan` runs automatically:
- Issues that disappeared (no longer open in state) are moved to `superseded`, removed from queue and clusters
- Superseded entries older than 90 days are pruned
- Temporary skips that have exceeded their `review_after` scan count are resurfaced back into the queue
- Epic clusters (`epic/*`) that become empty are deleted

---

## Key Data Structures

**`PlanModel.epic_triage_meta`** (`EpicTriageMeta`):
- `triage_stages`: dict of `{stage_name: TriageStagePayload}` — in-progress work
- `triaged_ids`: list of issue IDs covered by last completed triage
- `issue_snapshot_hash`: hash of open review IDs at last completion (used to detect new issues)
- `strategy_summary`: the execution plan from last `--complete`
- `last_triage`: archived copy of stages from last completed triage
- `last_completed_at`: timestamp

**`Cluster`** fields used in triage:
- `thesis`: one imperative sentence stating the cluster's goal
- `direction`: one of `delete, merge, flatten, enforce, simplify, decompose, extract, inline`
- `root_cause`: why this cluster exists
- `issue_ids`: list of issue IDs in this cluster
- `action_steps`: list of concrete steps to execute
- `dependency_order`: integer determining cluster sequencing
- `agent_safe`: bool — can be executed mechanically vs needs human judgment
- `triage_version`: version number of triage that last updated this cluster

---

## Cascade Confirmation Rules

Stages have a strict dependency chain: `observe → reflect → organize → commit`. Confirming a stage invalidates confirmations on all later stages (`_cascade_clear_later_confirmations`). This prevents a scenario where an agent re-records an earlier stage but claims later stages are still valid without re-reviewing.

The `--attestation` flag provides an "inline confirm" shortcut: passing it when recording `reflect` will auto-confirm `observe`, and passing it when recording `organize` will auto-confirm `reflect`, reducing round-trips.

---

1→"""Handler for ``plan triage`` subcommand."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage import confirmations as _confirmations_mod
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage import display as _display_mod
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage import helpers as _helpers_mod
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage import _stage_completion_commands as _completion_mod
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage import _stage_flow_commands as _flow_mod
    14→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage import _stage_validation as _validation_mod
    15→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage_playbook import TRIAGE_CMD_OBSERVE
    16→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    17→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    18→    append_log_entry,
    19→    build_triage_prompt,
    20→    collect_triage_input,
    21→    detect_recurring_patterns,
    22→    extract_issue_citations,
    23→    load_plan,
    24→    save_plan,
    25→)
    26→
    27→_MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN = _confirmations_mod._MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN
    28→_validate_attestation = _confirmations_mod._validate_attestation
    29→_triage_coverage = _helpers_mod._triage_coverage
    30→
    31→
    32→_TRIAGE_SUBMODULES = (
    33→    _helpers_mod, _display_mod, _confirmations_mod,
    34→    _flow_mod, _completion_mod, _validation_mod,
    35→)
    36→
    37→
    38→def _sync_triage_module_bindings() -> None:
    39→    """Propagate monkeypatch-friendly bindings into split triage modules.
    40→
    41→    Each submodule imports ``command_runtime``, ``load_plan``, etc. at the
    42→    top level.  Tests monkeypatch those names on *this* handler module; this
    43→    function copies the (potentially patched) references into every submodule
    44→    that already has the name, so mocks propagate automatically.
    45→
    46→    Adding a new submodule: append it to ``_TRIAGE_SUBMODULES``.
    47→    Adding a new patchable function: add it to the ``bindings`` dict below
    48→    *and* import it at the top of this file — submodules that import the same
    49→    name will pick it up automatically via ``hasattr``.
    50→    """
    51→    bindings = {
    52→        "command_runtime": command_runtime,
    53→        "save_plan": save_plan,
    54→        "load_plan": load_plan,
    55→        "collect_triage_input": collect_triage_input,
    56→        "detect_recurring_patterns": detect_recurring_patterns,
    57→        "append_log_entry": append_log_entry,
    58→        "extract_issue_citations": extract_issue_citations,
    59→    }
    60→    for mod in _TRIAGE_SUBMODULES:
    61→        for name, fn in bindings.items():
    62→            if hasattr(mod, name):
    63→                setattr(mod, name, fn)
    64→
    65→
    66→def _cmd_triage_start(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    67→    """Manually inject triage stage IDs into the queue and clear prior stages."""
    68→    _sync_triage_module_bindings()
    69→    plan = load_plan()
    70→
    71→    if _helpers_mod._has_triage_in_queue(plan):
    72→        print(colorize("  Planning mode stages are already in the queue.", "yellow"))
    73→        meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    74→        stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
    75→        if stages:
    76→            print(
    77→                colorize(
    78→                    f"  {len(stages)} stage(s) in progress — clearing to restart.", "yellow"
    79→                )
    80→            )
    81→            meta["triage_stages"] = {}
    82→            _helpers_mod._inject_triage_stages(plan)
    83→            save_plan(plan)
    84→            append_log_entry(
    85→                plan,
    86→                "triage_start",
    87→                actor="user",
    88→                detail={"action": "restart", "cleared_stages": list(stages.keys())},
    89→            )
    90→            save_plan(plan)
    91→            print(colorize("  Stages cleared. Begin with observe:", "green"))
    92→        else:
    93→            print(colorize("  Begin with observe:", "green"))
    94→        print(colorize(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_OBSERVE}", "dim"))
    95→        return
    96→
    97→    _helpers_mod._inject_triage_stages(plan)
    98→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})
    99→    meta["triage_stages"] = {}
   100→    save_plan(plan)
   101→
   102→    append_log_entry(plan, "triage_start", actor="user", detail={"action": "start"})
   103→    save_plan(plan)
   104→
   105→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   106→    si = collect_triage_input(plan, runtime.state)
   107→    print(colorize("  Planning mode started (4 stages queued).", "green"))
   108→    print(f"  Open review issues: {len(si.open_issues)}")
   109→    print(colorize("  Begin with observe:", "dim"))
   110→    print(colorize(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_OBSERVE}", "dim"))
   111→
   112→
   113→def cmd_plan_triage(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   114→    """Run epic triage: staged workflow OBSERVE → REFLECT → ORGANIZE → COMMIT."""
   115→    _sync_triage_module_bindings()
   116→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   117→    state = runtime.state
   118→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   119→        return
   120→
   121→    if getattr(args, "start", False):
   122→        _cmd_triage_start(args)
   123→        return
   124→    if getattr(args, "confirm", None):
   125→        _confirmations_mod._cmd_confirm_stage(args)
   126→        return
   127→    if getattr(args, "complete", False):
   128→        _completion_mod._cmd_triage_complete(args)
   129→        return
   130→    if getattr(args, "confirm_existing", False):
   131→        _completion_mod._cmd_confirm_existing(args)
   132→        return
   133→
   134→    stage = getattr(args, "stage", None)
   135→    if stage == "observe":
   136→        _flow_mod._cmd_stage_observe(args)
   137→        return
   138→    if stage == "reflect":
   139→        _flow_mod._cmd_stage_reflect(args)
   140→        return
   141→    if stage == "organize":
   142→        _flow_mod._cmd_stage_organize(args)
   143→        return
   144→
   145→    if getattr(args, "dry_run", False):
   146→        plan = load_plan()
   147→        si = collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   148→        prompt = build_triage_prompt(si)
   149→        print(colorize("  Epic triage — dry run", "bold"))
   150→        print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   151→        print(f"  Open review issues: {len(si.open_issues)}")
   152→        print(f"  Existing epics: {len(si.existing_epics)}")
   153→        print(f"  New since last: {len(si.new_since_last)}")
   154→        print(f"  Resolved since last: {len(si.resolved_since_last)}")
   155→        print(colorize("\n  Prompt that would be sent to LLM:", "dim"))
   156→        print()
   157→        print(prompt)
   158→        return
   159→
   160→    _display_mod._cmd_triage_dashboard(args)
   161→
   162→__all__ = [
   163→    "_MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN",
   164→    "_triage_coverage",
   165→    "_validate_attestation",
   166→    "cmd_plan_triage",
   167→]

    23→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize

    37→from .stage_helpers import _unenriched_clusters
    38→
    39→
    40→def _print_stage_progress(stages: dict, plan: dict | None = None) -> None:
    41→    """Print the 4-stage progress indicator."""
    42→    print(colorize("  Stages:", "dim"))
    43→    for stage_name, label in TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS:
    44→        if stage_name in stages:
    45→            if stages[stage_name].get("confirmed_at"):
    46→                print(colorize(f"    \u2713 {label} (confirmed)", "green"))
    47→            else:
    48→                print(colorize(f"    \u2713 {label} (needs confirm)", "yellow"))
    49→        elif TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES[stage_name].issubset(stages):
    50→            print(colorize(f"    \u2192 {label} (current)", "yellow"))
    51→        else:
    52→            print(colorize(f"    \u25cb {label}", "dim"))
    53→
    54→    # Show enrichment gaps when in the organize stage
    55→    if plan and "reflect" in stages and "organize" not in stages:
    56→        gaps = _unenriched_clusters(plan)
    57→        manual = _manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
    58→        if not manual:
    59→            print(colorize("\n    No manual clusters yet. Create clusters and enrich them.", "yellow"))
    60→        elif gaps:
    61→            print(colorize(f"\n    {len(gaps)} cluster(s) need enrichment:", "yellow"))
    62→            for name, missing in gaps:
    63→                print(colorize(f"      {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
    64→            print(colorize(
    65→                f"      Fix: {TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ENRICH_COMPACT}",
    66→                "dim",
    67→            ))
    68→        else:
    69→            print(colorize(f"\n    All {len(manual)} manual cluster(s) enriched.", "green"))
    70→
    71→def _print_progress(plan: dict, open_issues: dict) -> None:
    72→    """Show cluster state and unclustered issues."""
    73→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    74→    # Only show clusters that actually have issues (hide empty/stale ones)
    75→    active_clusters = {
    76→        name: c for name, c in clusters.items()
    77→        if c.get("issue_ids")
    78→    }
    79→    if active_clusters:
    80→        print(colorize("\n  Current clusters:", "cyan"))
    81→        for name, cluster in active_clusters.items():
    82→            count = len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
    83→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    84→            steps = cluster.get("action_steps", [])
    85→            auto = cluster.get("auto", False)
    86→            tags: list[str] = []
    87→            if auto:
    88→                tags.append("auto")
    89→            if desc:
    90→                tags.append("desc")
    91→            else:
    92→                tags.append("no desc")
    93→            if steps:
    94→                tags.append(f"{len(steps)} steps")
    95→            else:
    96→                if not auto:
    97→                    tags.append("no steps")
    98→            tag_str = f" [{', '.join(tags)}]"
    99→            desc_str = f" \u2014 {desc}" if desc else ""
   100→            print(f"    {name}: {count} items{tag_str}{desc_str}")
   101→
   102→    all_clustered: set[str] = set()
   103→    for c in clusters.values():
   104→        all_clustered.update(c.get("issue_ids", []))
   105→    unclustered = [fid for fid in open_issues if fid not in all_clustered]
   106→    if unclustered:
   107→        print(colorize(f"\n  {len(unclustered)} issues not yet in a cluster:", "yellow"))
   108→        for fid in unclustered[:10]:
   109→            f = open_issues[fid]
   110→            dim = (f.get("detail", {}) or {}).get("dimension", "") if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else ""
   111→            short = short_issue_id(fid)
   112→            print(f"    [{short}] [{dim}] {f.get('summary', '')}")
   113→        if len(unclustered) > 10:
   114→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(unclustered) - 10} more", "dim"))
   115→    elif open_issues:
   116→        organized, total, _ = _triage_coverage(plan, open_review_ids=set(open_issues.keys()))
   117→        print(colorize(f"\n  All {organized}/{total} issues are in clusters.", "green"))

   119→def _print_reflect_result(
   120→    *,
   121→    issue_count: int,
   122→    recurring_dims: list[str],
   123→    recurring: dict,
   124→    report: str,
   125→    is_reuse: bool,
   126→    cleared: list,
   127→    stages: dict,
   128→) -> None:
   129→    """Print the reflect stage output including briefing box and next steps."""
   130→    print(colorize(
   131→        f"  Reflect stage recorded: {issue_count} issues, "
   132→        f"{len(recurring_dims)} recurring dimension(s).",
   133→        "green",
   134→    ))
   135→    if is_reuse:
   136→        print(colorize("  Reflect data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   137→        if cleared:
   138→            _print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   139→    else:
   140→        print(colorize("  Now confirm your strategy.", "yellow"))
   141→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan triage --confirm reflect", "dim"))

   149→
   150→    print()

   152→    for line in report.strip().splitlines():
   153→        print(colorize(f"  \u2502 {line}", "cyan"))
   154→    print(colorize("  \u2514" + "\u2500" * 57 + "\u2518", "cyan"))
   155→    print()
   156→    print(colorize(

   249→def _print_reflect_dashboard(si: object, plan: dict) -> None:
   250→    """Show completed clusters, resolved issues, and recurring patterns."""
   251→    # si is a TriageInput
   252→    completed = getattr(si, "completed_clusters", [])
   253→    resolved = getattr(si, "resolved_issues", {})
   254→    open_issues = getattr(si, "open_issues", {})
   255→
   256→    if completed:
   257→        print(colorize("\n  Previously completed clusters:", "cyan"))
   258→        for c in completed[:10]:
   259→            name = c.get("name", "?")
   260→            count = len(c.get("issue_ids", []))
   261→            thesis = c.get("thesis", "")
   262→            print(f"    {name}: {count} issues")
   263→            if thesis:
   264→                print(colorize(f"      {thesis}", "dim"))
   265→            for step in c.get("action_steps", [])[:3]:
   266→                print(colorize(f"      - {step}", "dim"))
   267→        if len(completed) > 10:
   268→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(completed) - 10} more", "dim"))
   269→
   270→    if resolved:
   271→        print(colorize(f"\n  Resolved issues since last triage: {len(resolved)}", "cyan"))
   272→        for fid, f in sorted(resolved.items())[:10]:
   273→            status = f.get("status", "")
   274→            summary = f.get("summary", "")
   275→            detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   276→            dim = detail.get("dimension", "")
   277→            print(f"    [{status}] [{dim}] {summary}")
   278→            print(colorize(f"      {fid}", "dim"))
   279→        if len(resolved) > 10:
   280→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(resolved) - 10} more", "dim"))
   281→
   282→    recurring = detect_recurring_patterns(open_issues, resolved)
   283→    if recurring:
   284→        print(colorize("\n  Recurring patterns detected:", "yellow"))
   285→        for dim, info in sorted(recurring.items()):
   286→            resolved_count = len(info["resolved"])
   287→            open_count = len(info["open"])
   288→            label = "potential loop" if open_count >= resolved_count else "root cause unaddressed"
   289→            print(colorize(
   290→                f"    {dim}: {resolved_count} resolved, {open_count} still open — {label}",
   291→                "yellow",
   292→            ))
   293→    elif not completed and not resolved:
   294→        print(colorize("\n  First triage — no prior work to compare against.", "dim"))
   295→        print(colorize("  Focus your reflect report on your strategy:", "yellow"))
   296→        print(colorize("  - How will you resolve contradictions you identified in observe?", "dim"))
   297→        print(colorize("  - Which issues will you cluster together vs defer?", "dim"))
   298→        print(colorize("  - What's the overall arc of work and why?", "dim"))
   299→
   300→def _cmd_triage_dashboard(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   301→    """Default view: show issues, stage progress, and next command."""
   302→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   303→    state = runtime.state
   304→    plan = load_plan()
   305→    si = collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   306→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   307→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   308→
   309→    print(colorize("  Epic triage \u2014 manual", "bold"))
   310→    print(colorize("  " + "\u2500" * 60, "dim"))
   311→    print(f"  Open review issues: {len(si.open_issues)}")
   312→    print(colorize("  Goal: identify contradictions, resolve them, then group the coherent", "cyan"))
   313→    print(colorize("  remainder into clusters by root cause with action steps and priorities.", "cyan"))
   314→    if si.existing_epics:
   315→        print(f"  Existing epics: {len(si.existing_epics)}")
   316→    if si.new_since_last:
   317→        print(colorize(f"  New since last triage: {len(si.new_since_last)}", "yellow"))
   318→        for fid in sorted(si.new_since_last):
   319→            f = si.open_issues.get(fid, {})
   320→            dim = ""
   321→            detail = f.get("detail")
   322→            if isinstance(detail, dict):
   323→                dim = detail.get("dimension", "")
   324→            dim_tag = f" ({dim})" if dim else ""
   325→            print(colorize(f"    * [{short_issue_id(fid)}] {f.get('summary', '')}{dim_tag}", "yellow"))
   326→    if si.resolved_since_last:
   327→        print(f"  Resolved since last triage: {len(si.resolved_since_last)}")
   328→
   329→    # Stage progress (with enrichment gaps)
   330→    print()
   331→    _print_stage_progress(stages, plan)
   332→    if meta.get("stage_refresh_required"):
   333→        print(
   334→            colorize(
   335→                "  Note: review issues changed since stage progress started; "
   336→                "refresh stage reports before completion.",
   337→                "yellow",
   338→            )
   339→        )
   340→
   341→    # --- Action guidance (shown early so agents see what to do first) ---
   342→    print()
   343→    has_only_additions = bool(si.new_since_last) and not si.resolved_since_last
   344→    if "observe" not in stages and has_only_additions and meta.get("strategy_summary"):
   345→        # Show both paths: accept or re-plan
   346→        print(colorize("  Two paths available:", "yellow"))
   347→        print()
   348→        print(colorize("  To accept current queue (new items at end):", "cyan"))
   349→        print(
   350→            '    desloppify plan triage --confirm-existing '
   351→            '--note "..." --strategy "same" --confirmed "I have reviewed..."'
   352→        )
   353→        print()
   354→        print(colorize("  To re-prioritize and restructure:", "cyan"))
   355→        print(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_OBSERVE}")
   356→    elif "observe" not in stages:
   357→        print(colorize("  Next step:", "yellow"))
   358→        print(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_OBSERVE}")
   359→        print(colorize("    (themes, root causes, contradictions between issues — NOT a list of IDs)", "dim"))
   360→    elif "reflect" not in stages:
   361→        print(colorize("  Next step: use the completed work and patterns below to write your reflect report.", "yellow"))
   362→        print(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_REFLECT}")
   363→        print(colorize("    (Contradictions, recurring patterns, which direction to take, what to defer)", "dim"))
   364→    elif "organize" not in stages:
   365→        gaps = _unenriched_clusters(plan)
   366→        manual = _manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   367→
   368→        if not manual:
   369→            print(colorize("  Next steps:", "yellow"))
   370→            print("    0. Defer contradictory issues: `desloppify plan skip <hash>`")
   371→            print(f"    1. Create clusters:  {TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_CREATE}")
   372→            print(f"    2. Add issues:     {TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ADD}")
   373→            print(f"    3. Enrich clusters:  {TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_STEPS}")
   374→            print(f"    4. Record stage:     {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}")
   375→        elif gaps:
   376→            print(colorize("  Enrich these clusters before recording organize:", "yellow"))
   377→            for name, missing in gaps:
   378→                print(colorize(f"    {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
   379→            print(colorize(
   380→                f"    Fix: {TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ENRICH_COMPACT}",
   381→                "dim",
   382→            ))
   383→            print(colorize(f"    Then: {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   384→        else:
   385→            print(colorize("  All clusters enriched! Record the organize stage:", "green"))
   386→            print(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE}")
   387→
   388→        if meta.get("strategy_summary"):
   389→            print()
   390→            print(colorize("  Or fast-track (if existing plan is still valid):", "dim"))
   391→            print(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_CONFIRM_EXISTING}")
   392→    else:
   393→        print(colorize("  Ready to complete:", "green"))
   394→        print(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_COMPLETE_VERBOSE}")
   395→        print(colorize('    (use --strategy "same" to keep existing strategy)', "dim"))
   396→
   397→    # --- Prior stage reports (context for current action) ---
   398→    if "observe" in stages:
   399→        obs_report = stages["observe"].get("report", "")
   400→        if obs_report:
   401→            print(colorize("\n  Your observe analysis:", "dim"))
   402→            for line in obs_report.strip().splitlines()[:8]:
   403→                print(colorize(f"    {line}", "dim"))
   404→            if len(obs_report.strip().splitlines()) > 8:
   405→                print(colorize("    ...", "dim"))
   406→    if "reflect" in stages:
   407→        ref_report = stages["reflect"].get("report", "")
   408→        if ref_report:
   409→            print(colorize("\n  Your reflect strategy:", "dim"))
   410→            for line in ref_report.strip().splitlines()[:8]:
   411→                print(colorize(f"    {line}", "dim"))
   412→            if len(ref_report.strip().splitlines()) > 8:
   413→                print(colorize("    ...", "dim"))
   414→
   415→    # --- Issues data ---
   416→    # Group issues by dimension with suggestions to surface contradictions
   417→    by_dim: dict[str, list[tuple[str, dict]]] = defaultdict(list)
   418→    for fid, f in si.open_issues.items():
   419→        detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   420→        dim = detail.get("dimension", "unknown")
   421→        by_dim[dim].append((fid, f))
   422→
   423→    print(colorize("\n  Review issues by dimension:", "cyan"))
   424→    print(colorize("  (Look for contradictions: issues in the same dimension that", "dim"))
   425→    print(colorize("  recommend opposite changes. These must be resolved before clustering.)", "dim"))
   426→    max_per_dim = 5
   427→    for dim in sorted(by_dim, key=lambda d: (-len(by_dim[d]), d)):
   428→        items = by_dim[dim]
   429→        print(colorize(f"\n    {dim} ({len(items)}):", "bold"))
   430→        for fid, f in items[:max_per_dim]:
   431→            summary = f.get("summary", "")
   432→            short = short_issue_id(fid)
   433→            detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   434→            suggestion = (detail.get("suggestion") or "")[:120]
   435→            print(f"      [{short}] {summary}")
   436→            if suggestion:
   437→                print(colorize(f"        \u2192 {suggestion}", "dim"))
   438→        if len(items) > max_per_dim:
   439→            print(colorize(f"      ... and {len(items) - max_per_dim} more", "dim"))
   440→    print(colorize("\n  Use hash in commands: desloppify plan skip <hash>  |  desloppify show <hash>", "dim"))
   441→
   442→    # Show reflect dashboard when observe done, reflect not done
   443→    if "observe" in stages and "reflect" not in stages:
   444→        _print_reflect_dashboard(si, plan)
   445→
   446→    # Show current cluster progress
   447→    _print_progress(plan, si.open_issues)
   448→
   449→def _show_plan_summary(plan: dict, state: dict) -> None:
   450→    """Print a compact plan rendering: clusters + queue order + coverage."""
   451→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   452→    active = {
   453→        n: c for n, c in clusters.items()
   454→        if c.get("issue_ids") and not c.get("auto")
   455→    }
   456→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   457→
   458→    if active:
   459→        print(colorize(f"\n  Clusters ({len(active)}):", "bold"))
   460→        for name, cluster in active.items():
   461→            count = len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   462→            steps = len(cluster.get("action_steps", []))
   463→            desc = (cluster.get("description") or "")[:60]
   464→            print(f"    {name}: {count} items, {steps} steps — {desc}")
   465→
   466→    queue_order = [
   467→        fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   468→        if not fid.startswith("triage::") and not fid.startswith("workflow::")
   469→    ]
   470→    if queue_order:
   471→        show = min(15, len(queue_order))
   472→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue order (first {show} of {len(queue_order)}):", "bold"))
   473→        for i, fid in enumerate(queue_order[:show]):
   474→            f = issues.get(fid, {})
   475→            summary = (f.get("summary") or fid)[:60]
   476→            detector = f.get("detector", "?")
   477→            cn = _find_cluster_for(fid, active)
   478→            print(f"    {i+1}. [{detector}] {summary}{f' ({cn})' if cn else ''}")
   479→
   480→    organized, total, _ = _triage_coverage(
   481→        plan, open_review_ids=_open_review_ids_from_state(state),
   482→    )
   483→    pct = int(organized / total * 100) if total else 0
   484→    print(colorize(f"\n  Coverage: {organized}/{total} in clusters ({pct}%)", "bold"))
   485→
   486→__all__ = [
   487→    "_cmd_triage_dashboard",
   488→    "_print_organize_result",
   489→    "_print_progress",
   490→    "_print_reflect_dashboard",
   491→    "_print_reflect_result",
   492→    "_print_stage_progress",
   493→    "_show_plan_summary",
   494→]
   495→

    46→def _cmd_stage_observe(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    47→    """Record the OBSERVE stage: agent analyses themes and root causes.
    48→
    49→    No citation gate — the point is genuine analysis, not ID-stuffing.
    50→    Just requires a 100-char report describing what the agent observed.
    51→    """
    52→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
    53→
    54→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    55→    state = runtime.state
    56→    plan = load_plan()
    57→
    58→    # Auto-start: inject triage stage IDs if not present
    59→    if not _has_triage_in_queue(plan):
    60→        _inject_triage_stages(plan)
    61→        meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})
    62→        meta["triage_stages"] = {}
    63→        save_plan(plan)
    64→        print(colorize("  Planning mode auto-started (4 stages queued).", "cyan"))
    65→
    66→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})
    67→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
    68→    existing_stage = stages.get("observe")
    69→
    70→    # Jump-back: reuse existing report if no --report provided
    71→    report, is_reuse = _resolve_reusable_report(report, existing_stage)
    72→    if not report:
    73→        _print_observe_report_requirement()
    74→        return
    75→
    76→    si = collect_triage_input(plan, state)
    77→    issue_count = len(si.open_issues)
    78→
    79→    # Edge case: 0 issues
    80→    if issue_count == 0:
    81→        cleared = _record_observe_stage(
    82→            stages,
    83→            report=report,
    84→            issue_count=0,
    85→            cited_ids=[],
    86→            existing_stage=existing_stage,
    87→            is_reuse=is_reuse,
    88→        )
    89→        save_plan(plan)
    90→        print(colorize("  Observe stage recorded (no issues to analyse).", "green"))
    91→        if is_reuse:
    92→            print(colorize("  Observe data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
    93→            if cleared:
    94→                _print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
    95→        return
    96→
    97→    # Validation: report length (no citation counting)
    98→    min_chars = 50 if issue_count <= 3 else 100
    99→    if len(report) < min_chars:
   100→        print(colorize(f"  Report too short: {len(report)} chars (minimum {min_chars}).", "red"))
   101→        print(colorize("  Describe themes, root causes, contradictions, and how issues relate.", "dim"))
   102→        return
   103→
   104→    # Save stage (still extract citations for analytics, but don't gate on them)
   105→    valid_ids = set(si.open_issues.keys())
   106→    cited = extract_issue_citations(report, valid_ids)
   107→
   108→    cleared = _record_observe_stage(
   109→        stages,
   110→        report=report,
   111→        issue_count=issue_count,
   112→        cited_ids=sorted(cited),
   113→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   114→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   115→    )
   116→
   117→    save_plan(plan)
   118→
   119→    append_log_entry(
   120→        plan,
   121→        "triage_observe",
   122→        actor="user",
   123→        detail={"issue_count": issue_count, "cited_ids": sorted(cited), "reuse": is_reuse},
   124→    )
   125→    save_plan(plan)
   126→
   127→    print(
   128→        colorize(
   129→            f"  Observe stage recorded: {issue_count} issues analysed.",
   130→            "green",
   131→        )
   132→    )
   133→    if is_reuse:
   134→        print(colorize("  Observe data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   135→        if cleared:
   136→            _print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared, stages)
   137→    else:
   138→        print(colorize("  Now confirm your analysis.", "yellow"))
   139→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan triage --confirm observe", "dim"))
   140→
   141→
   142→def _cmd_stage_reflect(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   143→    """Record the REFLECT stage: compare current issues against completed work.
   144→
   145→    Forces the agent to consider what was previously resolved and whether
   146→    similar issues are recurring. Requires a 100-char report (50 if ≤3 issues).
   147→    If recurring patterns are detected, the report must mention at least one
   148→    recurring dimension name.
   149→    """
   150→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
   151→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
   152→
   153→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   154→    state = runtime.state
   155→    plan = load_plan()
   156→
   157→    if not _has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   158→        print(colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to reflect on.", "yellow"))
   159→        return
   160→
   161→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   162→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   163→
   164→    # Jump-back: reuse existing report if no --report provided
   165→    existing_stage = stages.get("reflect")
   166→    is_reuse = False
   167→    if not report and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("report"):
   168→        report = existing_stage["report"]
   169→        is_reuse = True
   170→    elif not report:
   171→        _print_reflect_report_requirement()
   172→        return
   173→
   174→    if "observe" not in stages:
   175→        print(colorize("  Cannot reflect: observe stage not complete.", "red"))
   176→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
   177→        return
   178→
   179→    si = collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   180→
   181→    # Fold-confirm: auto-confirm observe if attestation provided
   182→    if not _auto_confirm_observe_if_attested(
   183→        plan=plan,
   184→        stages=stages,
   185→        attestation=attestation,
   186→        triage_input=si,
   187→    ):
   188→        return
   189→
   190→    issue_count = len(si.open_issues)
   191→
   192→    # Validation: report length
   193→    min_chars = 50 if issue_count <= 3 else 100
   194→    if len(report) < min_chars:
   195→        print(colorize(f"  Report too short: {len(report)} chars (minimum {min_chars}).", "red"))
   196→        print(colorize("  Describe how current issues relate to previously completed work.", "dim"))
   197→        return
   198→
   199→    # Detect recurring patterns
   200→    recurring = detect_recurring_patterns(si.open_issues, si.resolved_issues)
   201→    recurring_dims = sorted(recurring.keys())
   202→
   203→    # If recurring patterns exist, report must mention at least one dimension
   204→    if not _validate_recurring_dimension_mentions(
   205→        report=report,
   206→        recurring_dims=recurring_dims,
   207→        recurring=recurring,
   208→    ):
   209→        return
   210→
   211→    # Save stage
   212→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   213→    reflect_stage = {
   214→        "stage": "reflect",
   215→        "report": report,
   216→        "cited_ids": [],
   217→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
   218→        "issue_count": issue_count,
   219→        "recurring_dims": recurring_dims,
   220→    }
   221→    stages["reflect"] = reflect_stage
   222→
   223→    # Jump-back: preserve or clear confirmation
   224→    if is_reuse and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("confirmed_at"):
   225→        stages["reflect"]["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
   226→        stages["reflect"]["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
   227→    cleared = _cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "reflect")
   228→
   229→    save_plan(plan)
   230→
   231→    append_log_entry(
   232→        plan,
   233→        "triage_reflect",
   234→        actor="user",
   235→        detail={
   236→            "issue_count": issue_count,
   237→            "recurring_dims": recurring_dims,
   238→            "reuse": is_reuse,
   239→        },
   240→    )
   241→    save_plan(plan)
   242→
   243→    _print_reflect_result(
   244→        issue_count=issue_count,
   245→        recurring_dims=recurring_dims,
   246→        recurring=recurring,
   247→        report=report,
   248→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   249→        cleared=cleared,
   250→        stages=stages,
   251→    )
   252→
   253→
   254→def _cmd_stage_organize(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   255→    """Record the ORGANIZE stage: validates cluster enrichment.
   256→
   257→    Instead of gating on a text report, validates that the plan data
   258→    itself has been enriched: each manual cluster needs description +
   259→    action_steps. This forces the agent to actually think about each
   260→    cluster's execution plan.
   261→    """
   262→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
   263→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
   264→
   265→    plan = load_plan()
   266→
   267→    if not _has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   268→        print(colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to organize.", "yellow"))
   269→        return
   270→
   271→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   272→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   273→
   274→    # Jump-back: reuse existing report if no --report provided
   275→    existing_stage = stages.get("organize")
   276→    is_reuse = False
   277→    if not report and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("report"):
   278→        report = existing_stage["report"]
   279→        is_reuse = True
   280→
   281→    if not _require_reflect_stage_for_organize(stages):
   282→        return
   283→
   284→    # Fold-confirm: auto-confirm reflect if attestation provided
   285→    if not _auto_confirm_reflect_for_organize(
   286→        args=args,
   287→        plan=plan,
   288→        stages=stages,
   289→        attestation=attestation,
   290→    ):
   291→        return
   292→
   293→    # Validate: at least 1 manual cluster with issues
   294→    manual_clusters = _manual_clusters_or_error(plan)
   295→    if manual_clusters is None:
   296→        return
   297→
   298→    # Validate: all manual clusters are enriched
   299→    if not _clusters_enriched_or_error(plan):
   300→        return
   301→
   302→    report = _organize_report_or_error(report)
   303→    if report is None:
   304→        return
   305→
   306→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   307→    cleared = _record_organize_stage(
   308→        stages,
   309→        report=report,
   310→        issue_count=len(manual_clusters),
   311→        existing_stage=existing_stage,
   312→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   313→    )
   314→
   315→    save_plan(plan)
   316→
   317→    append_log_entry(
   318→        plan,
   319→        "triage_organize",
   320→        actor="user",
   321→        detail={"cluster_count": len(manual_clusters), "reuse": is_reuse},
   322→    )
   323→    save_plan(plan)
   324→
   325→    _print_organize_result(
   326→        manual_clusters=manual_clusters,
   327→        plan=plan,
   328→        report=report,
   329→        is_reuse=is_reuse,
   330→        cleared=cleared,
   331→        stages=stages,
   332→    )
   333→
   334→
   335→__all__ = [
   336→    "_cmd_stage_observe",
   337→    "_cmd_stage_organize",
   338→    "_cmd_stage_reflect",
   339→]
   340→

    36→def _cmd_triage_complete(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    37→    """Complete triage — requires organize stage (or confirm-existing path)."""
    38→    strategy: str | None = getattr(args, "strategy", None)
    39→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
    40→    plan = load_plan()
    41→
    42→    if not _has_triage_in_queue(plan):
    43→        print(colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to complete.", "yellow"))
    44→        return
    45→
    46→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    47→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
    48→
    49→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    50→    review_ids = _open_review_ids_from_state(state)
    51→
    52→    # Require organize stage confirmed
    53→    if not _require_organize_stage_for_complete(
    54→        plan=plan,
    55→        meta=meta,
    56→        stages=stages,
    57→    ):
    58→        return
    59→
    60→    # Fold-confirm: auto-confirm organize if attestation provided
    61→    if not _auto_confirm_organize_for_complete(
    62→        plan=plan,
    63→        stages=stages,
    64→        attestation=attestation,
    65→    ):
    66→        return
    67→
    68→    # Re-validate cluster enrichment at completion time (prevents bypassing
    69→    # organize gate by editing plan.json directly)
    70→    if not _completion_clusters_valid(plan):
    71→        return
    72→
    73→    # Verify cluster coverage
    74→    organized, total, _clusters = _triage_coverage(plan, open_review_ids=review_ids)
    75→
    76→    if total > 0 and organized == 0:
    77→        print(colorize("  Cannot complete: no issues have been organized into clusters.", "red"))
    78→        print(colorize(f"  {total} issues are waiting.", "dim"))
    79→        return
    80→
    81→    if total > 0 and organized < total:
    82→        remaining = total - organized
    83→        print(
    84→            colorize(
    85→                f"  Warning: {remaining}/{total} issues are not yet in any cluster.",
    86→                "yellow",
    87→            )
    88→        )
    89→
    90→    strategy = _resolve_completion_strategy(strategy, meta=meta)
    91→    if strategy is None:
    92→        return
    93→    if not _completion_strategy_valid(strategy):
    94→        return
    95→
    96→    # Show summary
    97→    _print_complete_summary(plan, stages)
    98→
    99→    organized, total, _ = _triage_coverage(plan, open_review_ids=review_ids)
   100→
   101→    # Jump-back guidance before committing
   102→    print()
   103→    print(
   104→        colorize(
   105→            "  To revise an earlier stage: desloppify plan triage --stage <observe|reflect|organize>",
   106→            "dim",
   107→        )
   108→    )
   109→    print(colorize("  Pass --report to update, or omit to keep existing analysis.", "dim"))
   110→
   111→    append_log_entry(
   112→        plan,
   113→        "triage_complete",
   114→        actor="user",
   115→        detail={
   116→            "strategy_len": len(strategy.strip()),
   117→            "coverage": f"{organized}/{total}",
   118→        },
   119→    )
   120→
   121→    _apply_completion(args, plan, strategy)
   122→
   123→
   124→def _cmd_confirm_existing(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   125→    """Fast-track: confirm existing plan structure is still valid."""
   126→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
   127→    strategy: str | None = getattr(args, "strategy", None)
   128→    confirmed: str | None = getattr(args, "confirmed", None)
   129→    plan = load_plan()
   130→
   131→    if not _has_triage_in_queue(plan):
   132→        print(colorize("  No planning stages in the queue — nothing to confirm.", "yellow"))
   133→        return
   134→
   135→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   136→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   137→
   138→    # Require a prior completed triage — can't skip the full flow on first run
   139→    if not _require_prior_strategy_for_confirm(meta):
   140→        return
   141→
   142→    # Determine if this is a light-path (additions only) or full ceremony
   143→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   144→    state = runtime.state
   145→    si = collect_triage_input(plan, state)
   146→    has_only_additions = bool(si.new_since_last) and not si.resolved_since_last
   147→
   148→    if not _confirm_existing_stages_valid(
   149→        stages=stages,
   150→        has_only_additions=has_only_additions,
   151→        si=si,
   152→    ):
   153→        return
   154→
   155→    # Require existing enriched clusters
   156→    clusters_with_issues = _manual_clusters_with_issues(plan)
   157→    if not clusters_with_issues:
   158→        print(colorize("  Cannot confirm existing: no clusters with issues exist.", "red"))
   159→        print(colorize("  Use the full organize flow instead.", "dim"))
   160→        return
   161→
   162→    # Require note
   163→    if not _confirm_note_valid(note):
   164→        return
   165→
   166→    # Require strategy (default to "same" on light path)
   167→    strategy = _resolve_confirm_existing_strategy(
   168→        strategy,
   169→        has_only_additions=has_only_additions,
   170→        meta=meta,
   171→    )
   172→    if strategy is None:
   173→        return
   174→
   175→    # Strategy length check (unless "same")
   176→    if not _confirm_strategy_valid(strategy):
   177→        return
   178→
   179→    # Require --confirmed with plan review
   180→    confirmed_text = _confirmed_text_or_error(
   181→        plan=plan,
   182→        state=state,
   183→        confirmed=confirmed,
   184→    )
   185→    if confirmed_text is None:
   186→        return
   187→
   188→    # Validate: note cites at least 1 new/changed issue (if there are any)
   189→    if not _note_cites_new_issues_or_error(note, si):
   190→        return
   191→
   192→    # Record organize as confirmed-existing and complete
   193→    stages = meta.setdefault("triage_stages", {})
   194→    _record_confirm_existing_completion(
   195→        stages=stages,
   196→        note=note,
   197→        issue_count=len(clusters_with_issues),
   198→        confirmed_text=confirmed_text,
   199→    )
   200→
   201→    append_log_entry(
   202→        plan,
   203→        "triage_confirm_existing",
   204→        actor="user",
   205→        detail={"confirmed_text": confirmed_text},
   206→    )
   207→
   208→    _apply_completion(args, plan, strategy)
   209→    print(colorize("  Confirmed existing plan — triage complete.", "green"))
   210→
   211→
   212→__all__ = [
   213→    "_cmd_confirm_existing",
   214→    "_cmd_triage_complete",
   215→]

1→"""Attestation and confirmation handlers for plan triage."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     8→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
     9→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    10→    append_log_entry,
    11→    collect_triage_input,
    12→    detect_recurring_patterns,
    13→    load_plan,
    14→    save_plan,
    15→)

    17→

    19→from .helpers import (
    20→    _count_log_activity_since,
    21→    _observe_dimension_breakdown,
    22→    _open_review_ids_from_state,
    23→    _purge_triage_stage,
    24→    _triage_coverage,

    29→
    30→def _validate_attestation(
    31→    attestation: str,
    32→    stage: str,
    33→    *,
    34→    dimensions: list[str] | None = None,
    35→    cluster_names: list[str] | None = None,
    36→) -> str | None:
    37→    """Return error message if attestation doesn't reference required data, else None."""
    38→    text = attestation.lower()
    39→
    40→    if stage == "observe":
    41→        if dimensions:
    42→            found = [d for d in dimensions if d.lower().replace("_", " ") in text or d.lower() in text]
    43→            if not found:
    44→                dim_list = ", ".join(dimensions[:6])
    45→                return f"Attestation must reference at least one dimension from the summary. Mention one of: {dim_list}"
    46→
    47→    elif stage == "reflect":
    48→        refs: list[str] = []
    49→        if dimensions:
    50→            refs.extend(d for d in dimensions if d.lower().replace("_", " ") in text or d.lower() in text)
    51→        if cluster_names:
    52→            refs.extend(n for n in cluster_names if n.lower() in text)
    53→        if not refs and (dimensions or cluster_names):
    54→            return (
    55→                f"Attestation must reference at least one dimension or cluster name.\n"
    56→                f"  Valid dimensions: {', '.join((dimensions or [])[:6])}\n"
    57→                f"  Valid clusters: {', '.join((cluster_names or [])[:6]) if cluster_names else '(none yet)'}"
    58→            )
    59→
    60→    elif stage == "organize":
    61→        if cluster_names:
    62→            found = [n for n in cluster_names if n.lower() in text]
    63→            if not found:
    64→                names = ", ".join(cluster_names[:6])
    65→                return f"Attestation must reference at least one cluster from the plan. Mention one of: {names}"
    66→
    67→    return None
    68→
    69→def _confirm_observe(args: argparse.Namespace, plan: dict, stages: dict, attestation: str | None) -> None:
    70→    """Show observe summary and record confirmation if attestation is valid."""
    71→    if "observe" not in stages:
    72→        print(colorize("  Cannot confirm: observe stage not recorded.", "red"))
    73→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
    74→        return
    75→    if stages["observe"].get("confirmed_at"):
    76→        print(colorize("  Observe stage already confirmed.", "green"))
    77→        return
    78→
    79→    # Show summary
    80→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    81→    si = collect_triage_input(plan, runtime.state)
    82→    obs = stages["observe"]
    83→
    84→    print(colorize("  Stage: OBSERVE — Analyse issues & spot contradictions", "bold"))
    85→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 54, "dim"))
    86→
    87→    # Dimension breakdown
    88→    by_dim, dim_names = _observe_dimension_breakdown(si)
    89→
    90→    issue_count = obs.get("issue_count", len(si.open_issues))
    91→    print(f"  Your analysis covered {issue_count} issues across {len(by_dim)} dimensions:")
    92→    for dim in dim_names:
    93→        print(f"    {dim}: {by_dim[dim]} issues")
    94→
    95→    cited = obs.get("cited_ids", [])
    96→    if cited:
    97→        print(f"  You cited {len(cited)} issue IDs in your report.")
    98→
    99→    if not attestation or len(attestation.strip()) < _MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN:
   100→        if attestation:
   101→            print(colorize(
   102→                f"\n  Attestation too short ({len(attestation.strip())} chars, min {_MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN}).",
   103→                "red",
   104→            ))
   105→        print(colorize("\n  If satisfied, confirm:", "dim"))
   106→        print(colorize('    desloppify plan triage --confirm observe --attestation "I have thoroughly reviewed..."', "dim"))
   107→        print(colorize("  If not, continue reviewing issues before reflecting.", "dim"))
   108→        return
   109→
   110→    # Validate attestation references actual data
   111→    validation_err = _validate_attestation(attestation.strip(), "observe", dimensions=dim_names)
   112→    if validation_err:
   113→        print(colorize(f"\n  {validation_err}", "red"))
   114→        return
   115→
   116→    # Record confirmation
   117→    stages["observe"]["confirmed_at"] = utc_now()
   118→    stages["observe"]["confirmed_text"] = attestation.strip()
   119→    _purge_triage_stage(plan, "observe")
   120→    save_plan(plan)
   121→    append_log_entry(plan, "triage_confirm_observe", actor="user",
   122→                     detail={"attestation": attestation.strip()})
   123→    save_plan(plan)
   124→    print(colorize(f'  ✓ Observe confirmed: "{attestation.strip()}"', "green"))
   125→
   126→def _confirm_reflect(args: argparse.Namespace, plan: dict, stages: dict, attestation: str | None) -> None:
   127→    """Show reflect summary and record confirmation if attestation is valid."""
   128→    if "reflect" not in stages:
   129→        print(colorize("  Cannot confirm: reflect stage not recorded.", "red"))
   130→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage reflect --report "..."', "dim"))
   131→        return
   132→    if stages["reflect"].get("confirmed_at"):
   133→        print(colorize("  Reflect stage already confirmed.", "green"))
   134→        return
   135→
   136→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   137→    si = collect_triage_input(plan, runtime.state)
   138→    ref = stages["reflect"]
   139→
   140→    print(colorize("  Stage: REFLECT — Form strategy & present to user", "bold"))
   141→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 50, "dim"))
   142→
   143→    # Recurring dimensions
   144→    recurring = detect_recurring_patterns(si.open_issues, si.resolved_issues)
   145→    if recurring:
   146→        print(f"  Your strategy identified {len(recurring)} recurring dimension(s):")
   147→        for dim, info in sorted(recurring.items()):
   148→            resolved_count = len(info["resolved"])
   149→            open_count = len(info["open"])
   150→            label = "potential loop" if open_count >= resolved_count else "root cause unaddressed"
   151→            print(f"    {dim}: {resolved_count} resolved, {open_count} still open — {label}")
   152→    else:
   153→        print("  No recurring patterns detected.")
   154→
   155→    # Strategy briefing excerpt
   156→    report = ref.get("report", "")
   157→    if report:
   158→        print()
   159→        print(colorize("  ┌─ Your strategy briefing ───────────────────────┐", "cyan"))
   160→        for line in report.strip().splitlines()[:8]:
   161→            print(colorize(f"  │ {line}", "cyan"))
   162→        if len(report.strip().splitlines()) > 8:
   163→            print(colorize("  │ ...", "cyan"))
   164→        print(colorize("  └" + "─" * 51 + "┘", "cyan"))
   165→
   166→    # Collect data references for validation — include observe-stage dimensions
   167→    _by_dim, observe_dims = _observe_dimension_breakdown(si)
   168→    reflect_dims = sorted(set((list(recurring.keys()) if recurring else []) + observe_dims))
   169→    reflect_clusters = [n for n in plan.get("clusters", {}) if not plan["clusters"][n].get("auto")]
   170→
   171→    if not attestation or len(attestation.strip()) < _MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN:
   172→        if attestation:
   173→            print(colorize(
   174→                f"\n  Attestation too short ({len(attestation.strip())} chars, min {_MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN}).",
   175→                "red",
   176→            ))
   177→        print(colorize("\n  If satisfied, confirm:", "dim"))
   178→        print(colorize('    desloppify plan triage --confirm reflect --attestation "My strategy accounts for..."', "dim"))
   179→        print(colorize("  If not, refine your strategy before organizing.", "dim"))
   180→        return
   181→
   182→    # Validate attestation references actual data
   183→    validation_err = _validate_attestation(
   184→        attestation.strip(), "reflect",
   185→        dimensions=reflect_dims, cluster_names=reflect_clusters,
   186→    )
   187→    if validation_err:
   188→        print(colorize(f"\n  {validation_err}", "red"))
   189→        return
   190→
   191→    stages["reflect"]["confirmed_at"] = utc_now()
   192→    stages["reflect"]["confirmed_text"] = attestation.strip()
   193→    _purge_triage_stage(plan, "reflect")
   194→    save_plan(plan)
   195→    append_log_entry(plan, "triage_confirm_reflect", actor="user",
   196→                     detail={"attestation": attestation.strip()})
   197→    save_plan(plan)
   198→    print(colorize(f'  ✓ Reflect confirmed: "{attestation.strip()}"', "green"))
   199→
   200→def _confirm_organize(args: argparse.Namespace, plan: dict, stages: dict, attestation: str | None) -> None:
   201→    """Show full plan summary and record confirmation if attestation is valid."""
   202→    if "organize" not in stages:
   203→        print(colorize("  Cannot confirm: organize stage not recorded.", "red"))
   204→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan triage --stage organize --report "..."', "dim"))
   205→        return
   206→    if stages["organize"].get("confirmed_at"):
   207→        print(colorize("  Organize stage already confirmed.", "green"))
   208→        return
   209→
   210→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   211→    state = runtime.state
   212→
   213→    print(colorize("  Stage: ORGANIZE — Defer contradictions, cluster, & prioritize", "bold"))
   214→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 63, "dim"))
   215→
   216→    # Activity since reflect
   217→    reflect_ts = stages.get("reflect", {}).get("timestamp", "")
   218→    if reflect_ts:
   219→        activity = _count_log_activity_since(plan, reflect_ts)
   220→        if activity:
   221→            print("  Since reflect, you have:")
   222→            for action, count in sorted(activity.items()):
   223→                print(f"    {action}: {count}")
   224→        else:
   225→            print("  No logged plan operations since reflect.")
   226→
   227→    # Show full plan
   228→    print(colorize("\n  Plan:", "bold"))
   229→    _show_plan_summary(plan, state)
   230→
   231→    organize_clusters = [n for n in plan.get("clusters", {}) if not plan["clusters"][n].get("auto")]
   232→
   233→    if not attestation or len(attestation.strip()) < _MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN:
   234→        if attestation:
   235→            print(colorize(
   236→                f"\n  Attestation too short ({len(attestation.strip())} chars, min {_MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN}).",
   237→                "red",
   238→            ))
   239→        print(colorize("\n  If satisfied, confirm:", "dim"))
   240→        print(colorize('    desloppify plan triage --confirm organize --attestation "This plan is correct..."', "dim"))
   241→        print(colorize("  If not, adjust clusters, priorities, or queue order before completing.", "dim"))
   242→        return
   243→
   244→    # Validate attestation references actual data
   245→    validation_err = _validate_attestation(
   246→        attestation.strip(), "organize", cluster_names=organize_clusters,
   247→    )
   248→    if validation_err:
   249→        print(colorize(f"\n  {validation_err}", "red"))
   250→        return
   251→
   252→    organized, total, _ = _triage_coverage(
   253→        plan, open_review_ids=_open_review_ids_from_state(state),
   254→    )
   255→    stages["organize"]["confirmed_at"] = utc_now()
   256→    stages["organize"]["confirmed_text"] = attestation.strip()
   257→    _purge_triage_stage(plan, "organize")
   258→    save_plan(plan)
   259→    append_log_entry(plan, "triage_confirm_organize", actor="user",
   260→                     detail={
   261→                         "attestation": attestation.strip(),
   262→                         "coverage": f"{organized}/{total}",
   263→                     })
   264→    save_plan(plan)
   265→    print(colorize(f'  ✓ Organize confirmed: "{attestation.strip()}"', "green"))
   266→
   267→def _cmd_confirm_stage(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   268→    """Router for ``--confirm observe/reflect/organize``."""
   269→    confirm_stage = getattr(args, "confirm", None)
   270→    attestation = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
   271→    plan = load_plan()
   272→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   273→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   274→
   275→    if confirm_stage == "observe":
   276→        _confirm_observe(args, plan, stages, attestation)
   277→    elif confirm_stage == "reflect":
   278→        _confirm_reflect(args, plan, stages, attestation)
   279→    elif confirm_stage == "organize":
   280→        _confirm_organize(args, plan, stages, attestation)
   281→
   282→__all__ = [
   283→    "_MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN",
   284→    "_cmd_confirm_stage",
   285→    "_confirm_observe",
   286→    "_confirm_organize",
   287→    "_confirm_reflect",
   288→    "_validate_attestation",
   289→]
   290→

1→"""Terminal output helpers shared across commands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→import os
     7→import sys
     8→from collections.abc import Callable, Sequence
     9→from typing import Any
    10→
    11→LOC_COMPACT_THRESHOLD = 10000  # Switch from "1,234" to "1K" format
    12→
    13→COLORS = {
    14→    "reset": "\033[0m",
    15→    "bold": "\033[1m",
    16→    "dim": "\033[2m",
    17→    "red": "\033[31m",
    18→    "green": "\033[32m",
    19→    "yellow": "\033[33m",
    20→    "blue": "\033[34m",
    21→    "cyan": "\033[36m",
    22→}
    23→
    24→NO_COLOR = os.environ.get("NO_COLOR") is not None
    25→
    26→
    27→def colorize(text: str, color: str) -> str:
    28→    if NO_COLOR or not sys.stdout.isatty():
    29→        return str(text)
    30→    return f"{COLORS.get(color, '')}{text}{COLORS['reset']}"
    31→
    32→
    33→def log(msg: str) -> None:
    34→    """Print a dim status message to stderr."""
    35→    print(colorize(msg, "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
    36→
    37→
    38→def print_table(
    39→    headers: list[str], rows: list[list[str]], widths: list[int] | None = None
    40→) -> None:
    41→    if not rows:
    42→        return
    43→    if not widths:
    44→        widths = [
    45→            max(len(str(h)), *(len(str(r[i])) for r in rows))
    46→            for i, h in enumerate(headers)
    47→        ]
    48→    header_line = "  ".join(h.ljust(w) for h, w in zip(headers, widths, strict=False))
    49→    print(colorize(header_line, "bold"))
    50→    print(colorize("─" * (sum(widths) + 2 * (len(widths) - 1)), "dim"))
    51→    for row in rows:
    52→        print("  ".join(str(v).ljust(w) for v, w in zip(row, widths, strict=False)))
    53→
    54→
    55→def display_entries(
    56→    args: object,
    57→    entries: Sequence[Any],
    58→    *,
    59→    label: str,
    60→    empty_msg: str,
    61→    columns: Sequence[str],
    62→    widths: list[int] | None,
    63→    row_fn: Callable[[Any], list[str]],
    64→    json_payload: dict | None = None,
    65→    overflow: bool = True,
    66→) -> bool:
    67→    """Standard JSON/empty/table display for detect commands."""
    68→    if getattr(args, "json", False):
    69→        payload = json_payload or {"count": len(entries), "entries": entries}
    70→        print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2))
    71→        return True
    72→    if not entries:
    73→        print(colorize(empty_msg, "green"))
    74→        return False
    75→    print(colorize(f"\n{label}: {len(entries)}\n", "bold"))
    76→    top = getattr(args, "top", 20)
    77→    rows = [row_fn(e) for e in entries[:top]]
    78→    print_table(list(columns), rows, widths)
    79→    if overflow and len(entries) > top:
    80→        print(f"\n  ... and {len(entries) - top} more")
    81→    return True
    82→
    83→
    84→__all__ = [
    85→    "LOC_COMPACT_THRESHOLD",
    86→    "COLORS",
    87→    "NO_COLOR",
    88→    "colorize",
    89→    "display_entries",
    90→    "log",
    91→    "print_table",
    92→]
    93→

     7→from pathlib import Path

    10→from desloppify.app.output.tree_text import render_tree_lines
    11→from desloppify.app.output.visualize_helpers import (
    12→    _resolve_visualization_lang as _resolve_visualization_lang_impl,
    13→    build_dep_graph_for_path as _build_dep_graph_for_path_impl,
    14→    build_tree as _build_tree_impl,

    33→def _resolve_visualization_lang(path: Path, lang):

    39→

    43→

    52→
    53→def _write_visualization_output(output: Path, html: str) -> OutputResult:
    54→    """Write visualization HTML to disk using the shared output-result contract."""
    55→    try:
    56→        safe_write_text(output, html)
    57→    except OSError as exc:
    58→        return OutputResult(
    59→            ok=False,
    60→            status="error",
    61→            message=str(exc),
    62→            error_kind="visualization_write_error",
    63→        )
    64→    return OutputResult(ok=True, status="written", message=f"wrote {output}")
    65→
    66→
    67→def generate_visualization(
    68→    path: Path, state: dict | None = None, output: Path | None = None, lang=None
    69→) -> tuple[str, OutputResult]:
    70→    """Generate an HTML treemap visualization and explicit output result."""

    21→    OSError,

    26→def _resolve_visualization_lang(path: Path, lang):
    27→    """Resolve language config for visualization if not already provided."""
    28→    if lang:
    29→        return lang
    30→
    31→    search_roots = [path if path.is_dir() else path.parent]
    32→    search_roots.extend(search_roots[0].parents)
    33→    warned = False
    34→    for root in search_roots:
    35→        try:
    36→            detected = lang_api.auto_detect_lang(root)
    37→        except _RECOVERABLE_LANG_RESOLUTION_ERRORS as exc:
    38→            log_best_effort_failure(
    39→                logger,
    40→                f"auto-detect visualization language for {root}",
    41→                exc,
    42→            )
    43→            if not warned:
    44→                warned = True
    45→                warn_best_effort(
    46→                    "Could not auto-detect language plugins for visualization; "
    47→                    f"using fallback source discovery ({type(exc).__name__}: {exc})."
    48→                )
    49→            continue
    50→        if detected:
    51→            try:
    52→                return lang_api.get_lang(detected)
    53→            except _RECOVERABLE_LANG_RESOLUTION_ERRORS as exc:
    54→                log_best_effort_failure(

    58→                )
    59→                if not warned:

    61→                    warn_best_effort(
    62→                        "Visualization language plugin failed to load; using fallback source discovery "
    63→                        f"({type(exc).__name__}: {exc})."
    64→                    )
    65→                continue
    66→    return None

1→"""Shared display utilities for command output."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→
     6→def short_issue_id(fid: str) -> str:
     7→    """Extract the 8-char hash suffix from a issue ID for compact display.
     8→
     9→    Issue IDs look like ``review::.::holistic::dim::identifier::abcdef12``.
    10→    Commands accept the hash suffix as a shorthand for the full ID.
    11→    """
    12→    if "::" in fid:
    13→        suffix = fid.rsplit("::", 1)[-1]
    14→        if len(suffix) >= 8:
    15→            return suffix[:8]
    16→    return fid
    17→
    18→
    19→__all__ = ["short_issue_id"]
    20→
