1→"""Helper utilities for plan triage workflow."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from collections import defaultdict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     9→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    10→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    11→    TRIAGE_IDS,
    12→    TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
    13→    purge_ids,
    14→    review_issue_snapshot_hash,
    15→    save_plan,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.state import utc_now
    18→
    19→_STAGE_ORDER = ["observe", "reflect", "organize"]
    20→
    21→
    22→def _has_triage_in_queue(plan: dict) -> bool:
    23→    """Check if any triage stage ID is in the queue."""
    24→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    25→    return bool(order & TRIAGE_IDS)
    26→
    27→def _inject_triage_stages(plan: dict) -> None:
    28→    """Inject all 4 triage stage IDs into the queue (fresh start)."""
    29→    order: list[str] = plan.setdefault("queue_order", [])
    30→    existing = set(order)
    31→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
    32→        if sid not in existing:
    33→            order.insert(0 if sid == TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS[0] else len(order), sid)
    34→    # Re-insert in correct order at front
    35→    for sid in reversed(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS):
    36→        if sid in order:
    37→            order.remove(sid)
    38→    insert_at = 0
    39→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
    40→        order.insert(insert_at, sid)
    41→        insert_at += 1
    42→
    43→def _purge_triage_stage(plan: dict, stage_name: str) -> None:
    44→    """Purge a single triage stage ID from the queue."""
    45→    sid = f"triage::{stage_name}"
    46→    purge_ids(plan, [sid])
    47→
    48→def _cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages: dict, from_stage: str) -> list[str]:
    49→    """Clear confirmed_at/confirmed_text on stages AFTER *from_stage*. Returns cleared names."""
    50→    try:
    51→        idx = _STAGE_ORDER.index(from_stage)
    52→    except ValueError:
    53→        return []
    54→    cleared: list[str] = []
    55→    for later in _STAGE_ORDER[idx + 1:]:
    56→        if later in stages and stages[later].get("confirmed_at"):
    57→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_at", None)
    58→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_text", None)
    59→            cleared.append(later)
    60→    return cleared
    61→
    62→def _print_cascade_clear_feedback(cleared: list[str], stages: dict) -> None:
    63→    """Print yellow cascade-clear message with next-step guidance."""
    64→    if not cleared:
    65→        return
    66→    print(colorize(f"  Cleared confirmations on: {', '.join(cleared)}", "yellow"))
    67→    next_unconfirmed = next(
    68→        (s for s in _STAGE_ORDER if s in stages and not stages[s].get("confirmed_at")),
    69→        None,
    70→    )
    71→    if next_unconfirmed:
    72→        print(colorize(
    73→            f"  Re-confirm with: desloppify plan triage --confirm {next_unconfirmed}",
    74→            "dim",
    75→        ))
    76→
    77→def _observe_dimension_breakdown(si) -> tuple[dict[str, int], list[str]]:
    78→    """Count issues per dimension from a TriageInput. Returns (by_dim, sorted_dim_names)."""
    79→    by_dim: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
    80→    for _fid, f in si.open_issues.items():
    81→        detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
    82→        dim = detail.get("dimension", "unknown")
    83→        by_dim[dim] += 1
    84→    dim_names = sorted(by_dim, key=lambda d: (-by_dim[d], d))
    85→    return dict(by_dim), dim_names
    86→
    87→def _open_review_ids_from_state(state: dict) -> set[str]:
    88→    """Return IDs of all open review/concerns issues in state."""
    89→    return {
    90→        fid for fid, f in state.get("issues", {}).items()
    91→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
    92→    }
    93→
    94→def _triage_coverage(
    95→    plan: dict,
    96→    open_review_ids: set[str] | None = None,
    97→) -> tuple[int, int, dict]:
    98→    """Return (organized, total, clusters) for review issues in triage.
    99→
   100→    When *open_review_ids* is provided, use it as the full set of review
   101→    issues (from state) instead of falling back to queue_order.
   102→    """
   103→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   104→    all_cluster_ids: set[str] = set()
   105→    for c in clusters.values():
   106→        all_cluster_ids.update(c.get("issue_ids", []))
   107→    if open_review_ids is not None:
   108→        review_ids = list(open_review_ids)
   109→    else:
   110→        review_ids = [
   111→            fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   112→            if not fid.startswith("triage::") and not fid.startswith("workflow::") and (fid.startswith("review::") or fid.startswith("concerns::"))
   113→        ]
   114→    organized = sum(1 for fid in review_ids if fid in all_cluster_ids)
   115→    return organized, len(review_ids), clusters
   116→
   117→def _manual_clusters_with_issues(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   118→    """Return names of non-auto clusters that have issues."""
   119→    return [
   120→        name for name, c in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   121→        if c.get("issue_ids") and not c.get("auto")
   122→    ]
   123→
   124→def _apply_completion(args: argparse.Namespace, plan: dict, strategy: str) -> None:
   125→    """Shared completion logic: update meta, remove triage::pending, save."""
   126→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   127→    state = runtime.state
   128→
   129→    organized, total, clusters = _triage_coverage(
   130→        plan, open_review_ids=_open_review_ids_from_state(state),
   131→    )
   132→
   133→    # Purge all triage stage IDs.
   134→    purge_ids(plan, list(TRIAGE_IDS))
   135→
   136→    current_hash = review_issue_snapshot_hash(state)
   137→
   138→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_triage_meta", {})
   139→    meta["issue_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   140→    open_review_ids = sorted(
   141→        fid for fid, f in state.get("issues", {}).items()
   142→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   143→    )
   144→    meta["triaged_ids"] = open_review_ids
   145→    if strategy.strip().lower() != "same":
   146→        meta["strategy_summary"] = strategy
   147→    meta["trigger"] = "manual_triage"
   148→    meta["last_completed_at"] = utc_now()
   149→    # Archive stages before clearing so previous analysis is preserved
   150→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})

1→"""Shared helpers for consistent best-effort fallback behavior."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→import sys
     7→from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping
     8→from pathlib import Path
     9→
    10→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    11→
    12→
    13→def log_best_effort_failure(
    14→    logger: logging.Logger, action: str, exc: Exception
    15→) -> None:
    16→    """Record non-fatal fallback failures in a consistent debug format."""
    17→    logger.debug("Best-effort fallback failed while trying to %s: %s", action, exc)
    18→
    19→
    20→def print_error(message: str) -> None:
    21→    """Print a user-facing error message to stderr in a consistent format."""
    22→    print(colorize(f"  Error: {message}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
    23→
    24→
    25→def warn_best_effort(message: str) -> None:
    26→    """Emit a consistent user-facing warning for non-fatal fallback failures."""
    27→    print(colorize(f"  WARNING: {message}", "yellow"), file=sys.stderr)
    28→
    29→
    30→def print_write_error(
    31→    path: str | Path,
    32→    exc: Exception,
    33→    *,
    34→    label: str = "output",
    35→) -> None:
    36→    """Print a standardized write-failure error for command output files."""
    37→    print_error(f"Could not write {label} to {path}: {exc}")
    38→
    39→
    40→def restore_files_best_effort(
    41→    snapshots: Mapping[str, str],
    42→    write_fn: Callable[[str, str], None],
    43→) -> list[str]:
    44→    """Attempt restoring a set of files and return paths that failed to restore."""
    45→    failed: list[str] = []
    46→    for filepath, original in snapshots.items():
    47→        try:
    48→            write_fn(filepath, original)
    49→        except OSError:
    50→            failed.append(filepath)
    51→    return failed
    52→
    53→
    54→__all__ = [
    55→    "log_best_effort_failure",
    56→    "print_error",
    57→    "print_write_error",
    58→    "restore_files_best_effort",
    59→    "warn_best_effort",
    60→]
    61→

## Summary of Triage Stage Output Display in Desloppify

Based on my thorough review of the codebase, here's a comprehensive breakdown of how triage stage output is currently displayed to users:

### 1. **File Structure & Organization**

The triage system is organized in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/triage/` with these key files:
- `display.py` — main dashboard and result rendering
- `_stage_flow_commands.py` — observe/reflect/organize command handlers
- `_stage_completion_commands.py` — completion and confirmation handlers
- `confirmations.py` — confirmation/attestation display and validation
- `_stage_rendering.py` — requirement messages for stages
- `helpers.py` — utility functions for coverage, clustering, etc.
- `triage_playbook.py` — canonical commands and stage labels (moved to root triage_playbook)

### 2. **Core Output Functions Used**

**From `base/output/terminal.py`:**
```python
- colorize(text: str, color: str) -> str
  # Colors: "reset", "bold", "dim", "red", "green", "yellow", "blue", "cyan"
  # Respects NO_COLOR env var and checks sys.stdout.isatty()

- log(msg: str) -> None  
  # Prints dim message to stderr

- print_table(headers, rows, widths) 
  # Formats structured table output

- display_entries(args, entries, label, empty_msg, columns, widths, row_fn)
  # Standard JSON/table display for detect commands
```

**From `base/output/fallbacks.py`:**
```python
- print_error(message: str) -> None  # Red error to stderr
- warn_best_effort(message: str) -> None  # Yellow warning to stderr
- print_write_error(path, exc, label) -> None  # Standardized file write failure
- log_best_effort_failure(logger, action, exc) -> None  # Debug logging
```

**From `base/output/contract.py`:**
```python
- OutputResult(ok: bool, status: str, message: str | None, error_kind: str | None)
  # Dataclass for non-critical command side effects
```

### 3. **Exact Print Patterns Used in Triage Display**

**Dashboard (`_cmd_triage_dashboard` in display.py):**
```python
# Header with stage summary
print(colorize("  Epic triage — manual", "bold"))
print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))

# Issue counts
print(f"  Open review issues: {len(si.open_issues)}")
print(colorize("  Goal: identify contradictions, resolve them, then group...", "cyan"))

# Stage progress with symbols
print(colorize("  Stages:", "dim"))
for stage_name, label in TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS:
    if stage_name in stages and stages[stage_name].get("confirmed_at"):
        print(colorize(f"    ✓ {label} (confirmed)", "green"))
    elif ... not yet complete ...:
        print(colorize(f"    → {label} (current)", "yellow"))
    else:
        print(colorize(f"    ◯ {label}", "dim"))
```

**Boxed Output (for reflect/organize briefings):**
```python
print(colorize("  ┌─ Strategic briefing (share with user before organizing) ─┐", "cyan"))
for line in report.strip().splitlines():
    print(colorize(f"  │ {line}", "cyan"))
print(colorize("  └" + "─" * 57 + "┘", "cyan"))
```

### 4. **Stage-Specific Output Messages**

**OBSERVE Stage (`_stage_flow_commands.py`):**
```python
# Success
print(colorize(
    f"  Observe stage recorded: {issue_count} issues analysed.",
    "green",
))
# Guidance
print(colorize("  Now confirm your analysis.", "yellow"))
print(colorize("    desloppify plan triage --confirm observe", "dim"))
```

**REFLECT Stage (`_stage_flow_commands.py`):**
Uses `_print_reflect_result()` with:
- Success message in green
- Boxed strategic briefing in cyan
- IMPORTANT guidance in yellow:
  ```
  "IMPORTANT: Present your holistic strategy to the user. Explain:"
  "- What themes and root causes you see"
  "- What contradictions you found and which direction you'll take"
  "- What you'll prioritize, what you'll defer, the overall arc of work"
  "Wait for their input before creating clusters."
  ```
- Command references in dim

**ORGANIZE Stage (`_stage_flow_commands.py`):**
Uses `_print_organize_result()` with:
- Boxed prioritized organization in cyan
- IMPORTANT guidance in yellow about explaining each cluster and priority ordering
- Cluster summary with issue counts and step counts

**CONFIRMATION (`confirmations.py`):**
```python
# Per-stage confirmation display
print(colorize("  Stage: OBSERVE — Analyse issues & spot contradictions", "bold"))
print(colorize("  " + "─" * 54, "dim"))

# Summary data
print(f"  Your analysis covered {issue_count} issues across {len(by_dim)} dimensions:")
for dim in dim_names:
    print(f"    {dim}: {by_dim[dim]} issues")

# Guidance for confirmation
print(colorize('    desloppify plan triage --confirm observe --attestation "I have thoroughly reviewed..."', "dim"))
```

### 5. **"Present to User" / "Wait for Input" Instructions**

**Explicit textual instructions appear at:**

1. **Reflect stage** (line 157-175 in display.py):
   - Yellow colored: "IMPORTANT: Present your holistic strategy to the user. Explain:"
   - Yellow: "Wait for their input before creating clusters."

2. **Organize stage** (line 232-246 in display.py):
   - Yellow: "IMPORTANT: Present your prioritized organization to the user..."
   - Yellow: "which cluster comes first, second, third, what depends on what"

3. **Completion** (line 96-109 in _stage_completion_commands.py):
   - Dim: "To revise an earlier stage:" with command
   - Dim: "Pass --report to update, or omit to keep existing analysis."

4. **Dashboard action guidance** (line 341-395 in display.py):
   - Shows conditional paths ("Two paths available:", "Next step:")
   - Dim-colored command references

### 6. **Structured Output Block Patterns**

**Box patterns used:**
```python
# Briefing box (reflect)
┌─ Strategic briefing (share with user before organizing) ─┐
│ [report lines]
└───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

# Plan summary box (organize confirmation)
Plan:
  Clusters (N):
    name: X items, Y steps — description
  Queue order (first N of M):
    1. [detector] summary (cluster_name)
  Coverage: organized/total in clusters (pct%)
```

**Progress indicators:**
```
Stages:
  ✓ label (confirmed)         [green]
  → label (current)           [yellow]
  ◯ label                     [dim]
```

### 7. **Output Utilities in `/app/output/`**

**Currently available:**
- `visualize.py` — HTML treemap visualization generation
- `visualize_helpers.py` — data collection and tree building
- `tree_text.py` — LLM-readable tree text rendering
- `scorecard.py` — scoring/health visualization
- `_viz_cmd_context.py` — context loading for viz commands

**None of these are specifically used for triage output** — triage uses only the `base/output/terminal.py` colorize/print functions.

### 8. **Error Handling & Fallbacks**

**Pattern for errors:**
```python
print(colorize(f"  Report too short: {len(report)} chars (minimum {min_chars}).", "red"))
print(colorize("  Describe themes, root causes, contradictions...", "dim"))
return  # Exit early without completing stage
```

**Validation failures:**
```python
validation_err = _validate_attestation(attestation.strip(), "observe", dimensions=dim_names)
if validation_err:
    print(colorize(f"\n  {validation_err}", "red"))
    return
```

### 9. **Key Output Indentation Convention**

All triage output is indented with **two spaces** (`"  "`), except:
- Sub-items use **4-6 spaces** relative to content
- Boxed content maintains alignment with outer prefix

### 10. **Command Display Pattern**

Commands are shown in a standardized way:
```python
print(colorize(f"    {TRIAGE_CMD_OBSERVE}", "dim"))  # Indented, dim color
# Example output: "    desloppify plan triage --stage observe --report \"...\""
```

---

**Summary:** Triage output is simple, functional, and user-focused. It uses the `colorize()` function from `base/output/terminal.py` with carefully chosen colors (green for success, yellow for guidance/warnings, cyan for important context, dim for secondary info, red for errors). Messages prioritize clarity about what the user should do next, with explicit "Present to user" and "Wait for input" instructions. There are no fancy ASCII art or external output utilities—everything is plain-text print statements with color codes.
